(2) The same punishment as in paragraph 1 shall be imposed on whoever, against a group of persons because of their nationality, race, skin color, ethnic origin, age, health condition or gender, or with the intent to obtain for himself or for another unauthorized or disproportionate advantages

a) by violence or threat of violence unlawfully forces them to do something, omit something or tolerate something in violation of their fundamental human rights, or

b) tortures, torments or treats them in a similar other inhuman and cruel manner.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for seven years to twelve years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 or 2

a) and causes greater damage thereby,

b) on a protected person,

c) for a special motive, or

d) in a more serious manner of commission.

(4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for twelve years to twenty years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 or 2

a) and causes serious bodily harm or death thereby, or

b) and causes substantial damage thereby.

(5) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for twenty years to twenty-five years or by life imprisonment if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 or 2

a) and causes damage of large extent thereby,

b) and causes the death of multiple persons thereby, or

c) as a member of a dangerous grouping.

## § 191

(1) Whoever by force, threat of violence, or threat of other serious harm compels another to fulfill an obligation from a commitment to which a third person is otherwise entitled to performance, shall be punished by imprisonment for one year to three years.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three years to eight years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1

a) and causes greater damage thereby,

b) on a protected person,

c) from a special motive, or

d) by a more serious manner of conduct.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for seven years to fifteen years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1

a) and causes serious bodily harm or death thereby, or

b) and causes substantial damage thereby.

(4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for fifteen years to twenty-five years or by life imprisonment if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1

a) and causes large-scale damage thereby,

b) and causes the death of several persons thereby, or

c) as a member of a dangerous grouping.

## § 192

Coercion

(1) Whoever compels another to do something, omit something, or tolerate something, by abusing his material distress or urgent non-property need, or distress caused by his adverse personal circumstances, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one year to five years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1

a) by a more serious manner of conduct,

b) on a protected person,

c) from a special motive,

d) with the intent to obtain for himself or for another a greater property benefit or other benefit, or

e) by denying an employee in an employment or other similar employment relationship the right to ensure health and safety at work, to vacation for recreation, or to provision of legally guaranteed special working conditions for women and juvenile employees.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for four years to ten years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1

a) and causes serious bodily harm or death thereby, or

b) and causes substantial damage thereby.

(4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for ten years to twenty-five years or by life imprisonment if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1

a) and causes large-scale damage thereby,

b) and causes the death of several persons thereby,

c) as a member of a dangerous grouping, or

d) during a crisis situation.

## § 193

Restriction of freedom of religion

(1) Whoever by force, threat of violence, or threat of other serious harm

a) compels another to participate in a religious act,

b) without authorization prevents another from participating in a religious act, or

c) otherwise without authorization prevents another from exercising freedom of religion, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two years to six years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1

a) by a more serious manner of conduct,

b) on a protected person,

c) from a special motive, or

d) publicly.

## § 194

Violation of domiciliary freedom

(1) Whoever unlawfully enters the dwelling of another or unlawfully remains there shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one year to five years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1

a) by a more serious manner of conduct,

b) by overcoming an obstacle whose purpose is to prevent entry,

c) with at least two persons, or

d) from a special motive.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three years to eight years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2

a) against a protected person, or

b) as a member of a dangerous grouping.

## § 194a

Protection of privacy in dwelling

(1) Whoever intentionally violates another's right to his privacy in a dwelling, his right to his private and family life conducted in a dwelling, by without his consent acquiring for himself or other persons through unauthorized surveillance of his dwelling knowledge about his life and the life of persons staying in his dwelling, and by using information-technology means and other technical means produces from this observation records or other documentation, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to one year.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one year to two years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1

a) by a more serious manner of conduct,

b) by overcoming an obstacle whose purpose is to prevent entry into the dwelling,

c) with at least two persons, or

d) from a special motive.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two years to four years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1

a) against a protected person, or

b) as a member of a dangerous grouping.

## § 195

Violation of freedom of association and assembly

(1) Whoever by force, threat of violence, or threat of other serious harm restricts another in the exercise of his right to associate or right to assemble shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years.

(2) Whoever in connection with an assembly subject to notification obligation, by force or threat of imminent violence opposes the organizational measures of the convener or designated organizers of such assembly, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to one year.

Violation of the secrecy of transmitted messages

## § 196

(1) Whoever intentionally violates postal secrecy by discovering or opening a sealed letter or other document transmitted by a postal undertaking or by other usual means shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years.

(2) An employee of a postal service provider who commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 or intentionally enables another to commit such act, or alters or suppresses a document transmitted by a postal undertaking or by other usual means, shall be punished by imprisonment for one year to five years.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for four years to ten years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2

a) and causes substantial damage thereby,

b) from a special motive, or

c) by a more serious manner of conduct.

## § 197

(1) Whoever with the intent to cause damage to another or to obtain for himself or for another an unlawful benefit

a) discloses postal secrecy which he learned from a sealed letter or other document transmitted by a postal undertaking or by other usual means, which were not intended for him, or

b) uses such secrecy, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years.

(2) An employee of a postal service provider who commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 or intentionally enables another to commit such act, shall be punished by imprisonment for one year to five years.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for four years to ten years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2

a) and causes substantial damage thereby,

b) from a special motive, or

c) by a more serious manner of conduct.

Part Two

Criminal offenses against human dignity

## § 199

Rape

(1) Whoever by force or threat of imminent violence compels a woman to sexual intercourse or whoever abuses her defenselessness for such act shall be punished by imprisonment for five years to ten years.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for seven years to fifteen years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1

a) by a more serious manner of conduct,

b) on a protected person,

c) from a special motive,

d) on a woman in detention or serving a sentence of imprisonment, or

e) by joint conduct of at least two persons.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for fifteen years to twenty years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 and

a) causes serious bodily harm thereby, or

b) directly endangers the life of a child thereby.

(4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for twenty years to twenty-five years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1

a) and causes death thereby, or

b) during a crisis situation.

## § 200

Sexual Violence

(1) Who by violence or threat of immediate violence forces another to oral intercourse, anal intercourse or other sexual practices or who exploits his defenselessness for such an act, shall be punished by imprisonment for five to ten years.

(2) By imprisonment for seven to fifteen years the offender shall be punished if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1

a) by a more serious manner of acting,

b) on a protected person,

c) from a special motive,

d) on a person in custody or serving a sentence of imprisonment, or

e) by joint action of at least two persons.

(3) By imprisonment for fifteen to twenty years the offender shall be punished if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 and

a) causes thereby serious harm to health, or

b) directly endangers thereby the life of a child.

(4) By imprisonment for twenty to twenty-five years the offender shall be punished if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1

a) and causes thereby death, or

b) during a crisis situation.

Sexual Abuse

## § 201

(1) Who performs sexual intercourse with a person younger than fifteen years or who sexually abuses such person in another manner, shall be punished by imprisonment for three to ten years.

(2) By imprisonment for seven to twelve years the offender shall be punished if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1

a) by a more serious manner of acting,

b) on a protected person,

c) from a special motive, or

d) by joint action of at least two persons.

(3) By imprisonment for twelve to fifteen years the offender shall be punished if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 and

a) causes thereby serious harm to health, or

b) directly endangers thereby the life of a child.

(4) By imprisonment for fifteen to twenty years the offender shall be punished if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1

a) and causes thereby death, or

b) during a crisis situation.

## § 201a

Who through an electronic communication service proposes to a child younger than fifteen years a personal meeting with the intent to commit on him the criminal offense of sexual abuse or the criminal offense of production of child pornography, while not being a child himself, shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years.

## § 201b

(1) Who abuses a child younger than fifteen years with the intent to induce sexual gratification by his participation in sexual activities or sexual abuse, although such child need not directly participate in them, or who enables such abuse of him, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years.

(2) By imprisonment for one to five years the offender shall be punished if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 by joint action of at least two persons.

## § 202

(1) Who induces a child to extramarital intercourse or sexually abuses him in another manner, if

a) such child is entrusted to his care or under his supervision or a dependent person,

b) it concerns child prostitution, or

c) he abuses a recognized position which results from trust, authority or influence over the child, shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years.

(2) By imprisonment for two to eight years the offender shall be punished if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1

a) using coercion, or

b) by joint action of at least two persons.

(3) By imprisonment for three to ten years the offender shall be punished if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 and directly endangers thereby the life of a child.

## § 203

Sexual Intercourse between Relatives

(1) Who performs sexual intercourse with a relative in direct line or with a sibling, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years.

(2) By imprisonment for one to five years the offender shall be punished if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 on a protected person.

THIRD CHAPTER

CRIMINAL OFFENSES AGAINST FAMILY AND YOUTH

## § 204

Bigamy

(1) Who during the subsistence of his marriage enters into another marriage, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years.

(2) In the same manner as in paragraph 1 shall be punished who knowingly enters into marriage with a person who is already in another marriage.

## § 205

Abandoning a Child

(1) Who abandons a child whom he has a duty to care for and who cannot obtain help, in a place where he is not threatened by danger of endangerment of life or health, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years.

(2) By imprisonment for two to six years the offender shall be punished if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1

a) by a more serious manner of acting, or

b) on a child younger than three years.

## § 206

Desertion of a Child

(1) Who deserts a child whom he has a duty to care for and who cannot obtain help, and exposes him thereby to danger of death or harm to health, shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years.

(2) By imprisonment for three to ten years the offender shall be punished if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1

a) by a more serious manner of acting, or

b) on a child younger than six years.

(3) By imprisonment for seven to twelve years the offender shall be punished if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1

a) and causes thereby serious harm to health or death, or

b) during a crisis situation.

## § 207

Neglect of Mandatory Maintenance

(1) Who for at least two months in a period of two years fails to fulfill, even through negligence, the legal obligation to maintain or provide for another, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years.

(2) Who for at least two months in a period of two years intentionally evades the fulfillment of his legal obligation to maintain or provide for another, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years.

(3) By imprisonment for one to five years the offender shall be punished if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2

a) and exposes the entitled person to danger of need,

b) by a more serious manner of acting, or

c) although he was in the preceding twenty-four months convicted for such act or released from serving a sentence of imprisonment imposed for such act.

## § 208

Abuse of a Close Person and Entrusted Person

(1) Who to a close person or a person who is in his care or upbringing causes physical suffering or psychological suffering

a) by beating, kicking, hitting, causing wounds and burns of various kinds, humiliation, contemptuous treatment, constant surveillance, threatening, causing fear or stress, forced isolation, emotional blackmail or other behavior that endangers her physical or mental health or restricts her safety,

b) by unjustified denial of food, rest or sleep or denial of necessary personal care, clothing, hygiene, medical care, housing, upbringing or education,

c) by forcing to beg or to repeatedly perform an activity requiring her disproportionate physical strain or psychological strain with regard to her age or state of health or capable of damaging her health,

d) by exposing to the influence of substances capable of damaging her health, or

e) by unjustified restriction in access to property which she has the right to use, shall be punished by imprisonment for three to eight years.

(2) In the same manner as in paragraph 1 shall be punished who commits a similar act as is the act referred to in paragraph 1, although he was punished for a similar act in the preceding twelve months.

(3) By imprisonment for seven to fifteen years the offender shall be punished if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1

a) and causes thereby serious harm to health or death,

b) from a special motive,

c) although he was in the preceding twenty-four months convicted for such act or released from serving a sentence of imprisonment imposed for such act, or

d) by a more serious manner of acting.

(4) By imprisonment for fifteen to twenty-five years or a sentence of life imprisonment the offender shall be punished if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 and causes thereby serious harm to health to several persons or death of several persons.

Abduction

## § 209

(1) Whoever removes a child or a person suffering from a mental disorder or mentally underdeveloped from the care of a person who has the duty according to law or official decision to care for them, shall be punished by imprisonment for three to eight years.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for four to ten years if they commit the act specified in paragraph 1

a) in a more serious manner of conduct,

b) from a specific motive, or

c) and thereby endangers the moral development of the abducted person.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for seven to fifteen years if they commit the act specified in paragraph 1

a) with the intent to take such person abroad,

b) with the intent to obtain for themselves or another a substantial benefit, or

c) and thereby causes serious harm to health or death, or another particularly serious consequence.

(4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for fifteen to twenty-five years or by life imprisonment if they commit the act specified in paragraph 1

a) as a member of a dangerous group, or

b) with the intent to obtain for themselves or another a benefit of large extent.

## § 210

(1) Whoever, as a parent or relative in direct line, removes a child or a person suffering from a mental disorder or mentally underdeveloped from the care of a person who has the duty according to law or official decision to care for them, shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one year to five years if they commit the act specified in paragraph 1

a) in a more serious manner of conduct, or

b) from a specific motive.

## § 211

Endangering the Moral Education of Youth

(1) Whoever, even through negligence, exposes a person younger than eighteen years to the danger of demoralization by:

a) inducing them to an idle or immoral life,

b) enabling them to lead an idle or immoral life,

c) enabling them to commit acts that are criminal offences under this law,

d) enabling them to commit acts that are misdemeanors under special laws, or

e) preventing them from compulsory school attendance, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years.

(2) The same punishment as in paragraph 1 shall be imposed on whoever, in violation of generally binding legal regulations, employs a child younger than fifteen years, thereby preventing them from compulsory school attendance.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to five years if they commit the act specified in paragraph 1 or 2

a) in a more serious manner of conduct, or

b) from a specific motive.

FOURTH TITLE

CRIMINAL OFFENCES AGAINST PROPERTY

## § 212

Theft

(1) Whoever appropriates another's property by taking possession of it and

a) thereby causes minor damage,

b) commits the act by breaking in,

c) immediately after the act attempts to retain the property by violence or threat of imminent violence,

d) commits the act on a thing that another has on their person or with them,

e) such thing is a thing from the harvest from land that belongs to the agricultural land fund, or wood located on forest land, or fish from a pond with intensive breeding,

f) commits the act on a thing whose removal is subject to payment based on a special regulation, or

| | g) was penalized for a similar act in the preceding twelve months, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years if they commit the act specified in paragraph 1

a) and thereby causes greater damage,

b) although they were convicted for such act in the preceding twenty-four months, or

c) from a specific motive.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to ten years if they commit the act specified in paragraph 1

a) and thereby causes substantial damage,

b) in a more serious manner of conduct,

c) at a place enjoying piety or general respect or at a place of a public assembly or ceremony,

d) on a thing that enjoys protection under a special regulation,

e) by organizing such act, or

f) on a protected person.

(4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for ten to fifteen years if they commit the act specified in paragraph 1

a) and thereby causes damage of large extent,

b) as a member of a dangerous group, or

c) during a crisis situation.

## § 213

Embezzlement

(1) Whoever appropriates another's property that was entrusted to them and thereby causes minor damage to another's property, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one year to five years if they commit the act specified in paragraph 1

a) and thereby causes greater damage,

b) from a specific motive,

c) as a person who has a specially imposed duty to protect the interests of the injured party, or as a bankruptcy trustee, or

d) in a more serious manner of conduct.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to ten years if they commit the act specified in paragraph 1

a) and thereby causes substantial damage, or

b) on a protected person.

(4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for ten to fifteen years if they commit the act specified in paragraph 1

a) and thereby causes damage of large extent,

b) as a member of a dangerous group, or

c) during a crisis situation.

## § 214

Non-payment of Wages and Severance Pay

(1) Whoever, as a statutory body of a legal entity or a natural person who is an employer, or their proxy, fails to pay to their employee wages, salary or other remuneration for work, compensation for wages or severance pay, to which the employee is entitled, on the due date, although on this date they had financial resources for their payment, which they did not necessarily need to ensure the activity of the legal entity or the activity of the employer who is a natural person, or takes measures aimed at frustrating the payment of these financial resources, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one year to five years if they commit the act specified in paragraph 1

a) and thereby causes greater damage,

b) from a specific motive, or

c) against more than ten employees.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to eight years if they commit the act specified in paragraph 1 and thereby causes substantial damage.

(4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for seven to twelve years if they commit the act specified in paragraph 1 and thereby causes damage of large extent.

## § 215

Unauthorized Use of Another's Property

(1) Whoever takes possession of another's property of minor value with the intent to temporarily use it or whoever causes minor damage to another's property by temporarily using, without authorization, a thing that was entrusted to them, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to one year.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years if they commit the act specified in paragraph 1

a) and thereby causes greater damage or another particularly serious consequence,

b) from a specific motive, or

c) in a more serious manner of conduct.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one year to five years if they cause substantial damage by the act specified in paragraph 1.

(4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to eight years if they commit the act specified in paragraph 1

a) and thereby causes damage of large extent or another particularly serious consequence, or

b) as a member of a dangerous group.

Unauthorized Use of Another's Motor Vehicle

## § 216

(1) Whoever takes possession of another's motor vehicle of minor value with the intent to temporarily use it, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one year to five years if they commit the act specified in paragraph 1

a) and has already been convicted for such act,

b) from a specific motive,

c) in relation to a motor vehicle of greater value or by such act causes greater damage, or

d) in a more serious manner of conduct.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to eight years if they commit the act specified in paragraph 1 in relation to a motor vehicle of substantial value or by such act causes substantial damage.

(4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for seven to twelve years if they commit the act specified in paragraph 1

a) in relation to a motor vehicle of value of large extent or by such act causes damage of large extent, or

b) as a member of a dangerous group.

## § 217

(1) Whoever unlawfully uses another person's motor vehicle that was entrusted to them shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years if they commit the act specified in paragraph 1

a) and has already been convicted for such an act,

b) from a special motive,

c) in relation to a motor vehicle of greater value or causes greater damage by such act, or

d) in a more serious manner of acting.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one year to five years if they commit the act specified in paragraph 1 in relation to a motor vehicle of substantial value or causes substantial damage by such act.

(4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three years to eight years if they commit the act specified in paragraph 1

a) in relation to a motor vehicle of large-scale value or causes large-scale damage by such act, or

b) as a member of a dangerous grouping.

## § 218

Unlawful Interference with the Right to a House, Apartment or Non-residential Premises

(1) Whoever unlawfully occupies or uses another person's house or apartment or unlawfully prevents an authorized person from using a house or apartment shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years.

(2) Whoever unlawfully enters, occupies or uses another person's non-residential premises or unlawfully prevents an authorized person from using non-residential premises shall be punished by imprisonment for up to one year.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one year to five years if they commit the act specified in paragraph 1 or 2

a) in a more serious manner of acting, or

b) from a special motive.

## § 219

Unlawful Production and Use of a Payment Instrument

(1) Whoever unlawfully stores, transports, obtains or otherwise acquires or provides to another person a payment instrument shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years.

(2) Whoever unlawfully uses a payment instrument shall be punished by imprisonment for one year to five years.

(3) The same as in paragraph 2 shall be punished whoever counterfeits, alters, imitates or unlawfully produces a payment instrument or whoever stores, transports, obtains or otherwise acquires, uses or provides to another person such a payment instrument.

(4) Whoever produces, acquires for themselves or another person or stores a tool, computer program or other means specially adapted for committing the act specified in paragraph 3 shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years.

(5) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two years to eight years if they commit the act specified in paragraph 1, 2, 3 or 4

a) in a more serious manner of acting,

b) on a larger scale, or

c) from a special motive.

(6) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three years to ten years if they commit the act specified in paragraph 1, 2, 3 or 4 on a substantial scale.

(7) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for five years to twelve years if they commit the act specified in paragraph 1, 2, 3 or 4

a) on a large scale, or

b) as a member of a dangerous grouping.

## § 219a

Unlawful Production, Use or Storage of a Verification Mark of a Measuring Instrument or Security Mark of a Measuring Instrument

(1) Whoever unlawfully produces, alters, imitates, combines or counterfeits a verification mark of a measuring instrument or security mark of a measuring instrument for the purpose of using it as genuine or for such purpose transports it or provides it to another person or unlawfully stores verification marks of a measuring instrument or security marks of a measuring instrument for the purpose of using them as genuine shall be punished by imprisonment for one year to five years.

(2) Whoever unlawfully produces, stores, obtains or otherwise acquires, provides to another person or uses a stamp, tool or other means specially adapted for committing the act specified in paragraph 1 shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two years to eight years if they commit the act specified in paragraph 1 or 2

a) in a more serious manner of acting,

b) on a larger scale, or

c) from a special motive.

(4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for five years to twelve years if they commit the act specified in paragraph 1 or 2

a) on a large scale, or

b) as a member of a dangerous grouping.

## § 220

Falsification and Alteration of Identification Data of a Motor Vehicle

(1) Whoever falsifies or alters the identification number of a motor vehicle, the production number of the engine of a motor vehicle, the production plate of a motor vehicle or the production number of components of a motor vehicle or uses as genuine the identification number, production number of the engine of a motor vehicle, production plate or production number of components of a motor vehicle of another motor vehicle shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one year to five years if they commit the act specified in paragraph 1

a) and has already been convicted for such an act,

b) in relation to a motor vehicle of greater value or causes greater damage by such act, or

c) from a special motive.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three years to eight years if they commit the act specified in paragraph 1

a) in a more serious manner of acting, or

b) in relation to a motor vehicle of substantial value or causes substantial damage by such act.

(4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for four years to ten years if they commit the act specified in paragraph 1

a) in relation to a motor vehicle of large-scale value or causes large-scale damage by such act, or

b) as a member of a dangerous grouping.

## § 221

Fraud

(1) Whoever, to the detriment of another person's property, enriches themselves or another person by misleading someone or taking advantage of someone's error, and thus causes minor damage to another person's property, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one year to five years if they commit the act specified in paragraph 1 and cause greater damage thereby.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three years to ten years if they commit the act specified in paragraph 1

a) and cause substantial damage thereby,

b) from a special motive,

c) in a more serious manner of acting, or

d) on a protected person.

(4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for ten years to fifteen years if they commit the act specified in paragraph 1

a) and cause large-scale damage thereby,

b) as a member of a dangerous grouping, or

c) during a crisis situation.

## § 222

Credit Fraud

(1) Whoever fraudulently obtains from another person a credit or credit security by misleading them regarding the fulfillment of conditions for granting credit or for repaying credit, and thus causes minor damage to them, shall be punished by imprisonment for one year to five years.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two years to five years if, as an employee, member, statutory body, representative or other person authorized to act on behalf of the person granting credit, they enable the obtaining of credit by a person who they know does not meet the conditions determined for its granting.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three years to ten years if they commit the act specified in paragraph 1 or 2

a) and cause greater damage thereby,

b) from a special motive, or

c) in a more serious manner of acting.

(4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for five years to twelve years if they commit the act specified in paragraph 1 or 2 and cause substantial damage thereby.

(5) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for ten years to fifteen years if they commit the act specified in paragraph 1 or 2

a) and cause large-scale damage thereby,

b) as a member of a dangerous grouping, or

c) during a crisis situation.

## § 223

Insurance Fraud

(1) Whoever obtains insurance benefits from another by misleading them regarding the fulfillment of conditions for its provision, and thereby causes them minor damage, shall be punished by imprisonment for one year to five years.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two years to five years if, as an employee, member, representative or other person authorized to act on behalf of the provider of insurance benefits, they assist in obtaining insurance benefits for someone whom they know does not meet the conditions required for its provision.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three years to ten years if they commit the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2

a) and thereby causes greater damage,

b) from a special motive, or

c) in a more serious manner of conduct.

(4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for five years to twelve years if they commit the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2 and thereby causes substantial damage.

(5) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for ten years to fifteen years if they commit the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2

a) and thereby causes damage of a large extent,

b) as a member of a dangerous grouping, or

c) during a crisis situation.

## § 224

Capital Fraud

(1) Whoever, in connection with the offering, sale or distribution of securities or other instruments that promise participation in the property returns of an enterprise, or whoever, in connection with an offer to increase the returns of such investment, in prospectuses or in other promotional materials or overviews concerning the property relations or returns of the enterprise in relation to a larger number of persons, provides false information or unrealistic information about the returns of investment or about the property relations of the enterprise in which investment is to be made, or whoever conceals the disadvantages of such investment, shall be punished by imprisonment for one year to five years.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three years to ten years if they commit the act referred to in paragraph 1

a) and thereby causes greater damage,

b) from a special motive, or

c) in a more serious manner of conduct.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for five years to twelve years if they commit the act referred to in paragraph 1 and thereby causes substantial damage.

(4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for ten years to fifteen years if they commit the act referred to in paragraph 1

a) and thereby causes damage of a large extent,

b) as a member of a dangerous grouping, or

c) during a crisis situation.

## § 225

Subsidy Fraud

(1) Whoever obtains from another a grant, subsidy, contribution or other benefit from the state budget, from the budget of a public law institution, the budget of a state fund, the budget of a higher territorial unit or the budget of a municipality, the provision or use of which is, according to generally binding legal regulations, tied to conditions which they do not meet, by misleading them regarding their fulfillment, shall be punished by imprisonment for one year to five years.

(2) The same punishment as in paragraph 1 shall apply to whoever uses an obtained grant, subsidy, contribution or other benefit from the state budget, from the budget of a public law institution, the budget of a state fund, the budget of a higher territorial unit or the budget of a municipality for a purpose other than the designated one.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two years to five years if, as an employee, member, representative or other person authorized to act on behalf of the provider of a grant, subsidy, contribution or other benefit from the state budget, from the budget of a public law institution, the budget of a state fund, the budget of a higher territorial unit or the budget of a municipality, they enable the obtaining of a grant, subsidy, contribution or other benefit from the state budget, from the budget of a public law institution, the budget of a state fund, the budget of a higher territorial unit or the budget of a municipality by someone whom they know does not meet the conditions required for its provision.

(4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three years to ten years if they commit the act referred to in paragraph 1, 2 or 3

a) and thereby causes greater damage,

b) from a special motive, or

c) in a more serious manner of conduct.

(5) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for five years to twelve years if they commit the act referred to in paragraph 1, 2 or 3 and thereby causes substantial damage.

(6) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for ten years to fifteen years if they commit the act referred to in paragraph 1, 2 or 3

a) and thereby causes damage of a large extent,

b) as a member of a dangerous grouping, or

c) during a crisis situation.

## § 226

Unauthorized Enrichment

(1) Whoever, to the detriment of another's property, enriches themselves or another by unauthorized interference with the technical or software equipment of a computer, vending machine or other similar device or technical equipment serving for the automated execution of the sale of goods, exchange or withdrawal of money or for the provision of paid performances, services, information or other benefits, achieves that goods, services or information are obtained without the required payment or that money is obtained without authorization, and thereby causes minor damage to another's property, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years if they commit the act referred to in paragraph 1

a) and thereby causes greater damage,

b) from a special motive, or

c) in a more serious manner of conduct.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three years to eight years if they commit the act referred to in paragraph 1 and thereby causes substantial damage.

(4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for seven years to twelve years if they commit the act referred to in paragraph 1

a) and thereby causes damage of a large extent,

b) as a member of a dangerous grouping, or

c) during a crisis situation.

## § 227

Fraudulent Bankruptcy

(1) Whoever, with the intent to cause damage to another or to procure for themselves or another an unauthorized benefit, causes the bankruptcy of a legal entity in which they are a statutory body or authorized representative, by using the property of this legal entity even only partially

a) to establish another legal entity, or

b) to acquire its property interest in another legal entity, or whoever acts in such a way with the intent to cause the bankruptcy of a legal entity in which they are a statutory body or authorized representative, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one year to five years if they commit the act referred to in paragraph 1 and thereby causes greater damage or thereby obtains for themselves or for another a greater benefit.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two years to eight years if they commit the act referred to in paragraph 1

a) and thereby causes substantial damage or thereby obtains for themselves or for another a substantial benefit, or

b) in a more serious manner of conduct.

(4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for five years to twelve years if they commit the act referred to in paragraph 1

a) and thereby causes damage of a large extent or thereby obtains for themselves or for another a benefit of a large extent, or

b) and thereby causes another's bankruptcy.

## § 228

Culpable Bankruptcy

(1) Whoever causes the bankruptcy of a legal entity and to a greater extent frustrates the satisfaction of its creditor by

a) investing in a loss-making business without corresponding financial coverage,

b) accepting or providing a loan that is disadvantageous from the perspective of the financial possibilities of the legal entity,

c) destroying, damaging, donating, concealing or otherwise removing, even only partially, the property of this legal entity to the detriment of the creditor of the legal entity,

d) using a greater part of the income from the business of the legal entity for their own personal consumption, or

e) depositing income from the business of the legal entity, even only partially, in lotteries, games or bets, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years.

(2) The same punishment as in paragraph 1 shall apply to whoever acts in the manner referred to in paragraph 1 letters a) to e), and the bankruptcy of the legal entity was averted only by the provision of a contribution or other measure of a state body, territorial self-government or public law institution, to which these bodies were not obliged.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one year to five years if they commit the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2 and thereby causes substantial damage.

(4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three years to eight years if they commit the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2

a) and thereby causes damage of a large extent, or

b) and thereby causes another's bankruptcy.

## § 229

Operating unfair games and bets

(1) Whoever operates a monetary or other similar game or bet whose rules do not guarantee equal chances of winning to all participants shall be punished by imprisonment for one year to five years.

(2) By imprisonment for four years to eight years the offender shall be punished if they commit the act specified in paragraph 1

a) and thereby cause greater damage, or

b) from a special motive.

(3) By imprisonment for seven years to twelve years the offender shall be punished if they commit the act specified in paragraph 1

a) and thereby cause substantial damage, or

b) by a more serious manner of acting.

(4) By imprisonment for twelve years to fifteen years the offender shall be punished if they commit the act specified in paragraph 1

a) and thereby cause damage of large extent, or

b) as a member of a dangerous grouping.

## § 230

Unauthorized operation of lotteries and other similar games

(1) Whoever operates a lottery or other similar game without authorization shall be punished by imprisonment for one year to five years.

(2) By imprisonment for four years to eight years the offender shall be punished if they commit the act specified in paragraph 1

a) and thereby cause greater damage, or

b) from a special motive.

(3) By imprisonment for seven years to twelve years the offender shall be punished if they commit the act specified in paragraph 1

a) and thereby cause substantial damage, or

b) by a more serious manner of acting.

(4) By imprisonment for twelve years to fifteen years the offender shall be punished if they commit the act specified in paragraph 1

a) and thereby cause damage of large extent, or

b) as a member of a dangerous grouping.

Legalization of proceeds from criminal activity

## § 233

(1) Whoever acquires, keeps or uses a thing which is a proceed from criminal activity committed by another person on the territory of the Slovak Republic or abroad shall be punished by imprisonment for two years to five years.

(2) The same as in paragraph 1 shall be punished the one who

a) conceals, transfers to themselves or another a thing which is a proceed from criminal activity committed on the territory of the Slovak Republic or abroad,

b) changes the nature of a thing which is a proceed from criminal activity committed on the territory of the Slovak Republic or abroad, deposits it or otherwise disposes of it with the intention to enable themselves or another to escape criminal prosecution, punishment or protective measure or their execution, or

c) conceals the existence of a thing which is a proceed from criminal activity committed on the territory of the Slovak Republic or abroad, in particular by concealing its origin in criminal activity, its location or ownership right or other right to it.

(3) By imprisonment for three years to eight years the offender shall be punished if they commit the act specified in paragraph 1 or 2

a) from a special motive, or

b) to a greater extent.

(4) By imprisonment for seven years to twelve years the offender shall be punished if they commit the act specified in paragraph 1 or 2

a) as a public official,

b) to a substantial extent, or

c) by a more serious manner of acting.

(5) By imprisonment for twelve years to twenty years the offender shall be punished if they commit the act specified in paragraph 1 or 2

a) to a large extent,

b) in relation to a thing originating from a particularly serious felony, or

c) as a member of a dangerous grouping.

## § 233a

(1) Whoever by negligence conceals, transfers to themselves or another, keeps or uses a thing of greater value which is a proceed from criminal activity committed by another person on the territory of the Slovak Republic or abroad shall be punished by imprisonment up to two years.

(2) The same as in paragraph 1 shall be punished whoever by negligence enables another to conceal the origin or detection of the origin of a thing of greater value which is a proceed from criminal activity committed on the territory of the Slovak Republic or abroad.

(3) By imprisonment for two years to five years the offender shall be punished if they commit the act specified in paragraph 1 or 2

a) in relation to a thing of substantial value, or

b) by a more serious manner of acting.

(4) By imprisonment for three years to eight years the offender shall be punished if they commit the act specified in paragraph 1 or 2 in relation to a thing

a) of value of large extent, or

b) originating from a particularly serious felony.

## § 234

(1) Whoever does not notify or report, despite the fact that such obligation arises from their employment, profession, position or function,

a) facts indicating that another committed a criminal offense of legalization of proceeds from criminal activity under § 233 or § 233a or a criminal offense of financing terrorism under § 419c, or

b) an unusual business transaction, shall be punished by imprisonment for two years to eight years.

(2) The act specified in paragraph 1 is not punishable if the offender could not make the notification or report without putting themselves or a close person into danger of criminal prosecution.

## § 235

Usury

(1) Whoever by abusing someone's distress, inexperience or mental weakness or someone's agitation, has provided or promised to themselves or another a performance whose value is in gross disproportion to the value of the reciprocal performance, or whoever asserts such a claim or transfers it to themselves with the intention to assert it, shall be punished by imprisonment for one year to five years.

(2) The same as in paragraph 1 shall be punished whoever provides performance without authorization, in violation of issued authorization or violates consumer rights protected by law and has provided or promised to themselves or another a performance whose value is in gross disproportion to the value of the reciprocal performance or whoever asserts such a claim or transfers it to themselves with the intention to assert it.

(3) By imprisonment for three years to eight years the offender shall be punished if they commit the act specified in paragraph 1 or paragraph 2

a) and thereby cause greater damage,

b) against a protected person, or

c) from a special motive.

(4) By imprisonment for five years to twelve years the offender shall be punished if they commit the act specified in paragraph 1 or paragraph 2

a) and thereby cause substantial damage, or

b) by a more serious manner of acting.

(5) By imprisonment for ten years to fifteen years the offender shall be punished if they commit the act specified in paragraph 1 or paragraph 2

a) and thereby cause damage of large extent, or

b) as a member of a dangerous grouping.

## § 236

Concealment of a thing

(1) Whoever appropriates another's thing of small value which came into their power by finding, by mistake or otherwise without the consent of the entitled person shall be punished by imprisonment up to one year.

(2) By imprisonment for six months to five years the offender shall be punished if they commit the act specified in paragraph 1 and thereby cause greater damage.

Breach of duty in administration of another's property

## § 237

(1) Whoever causes another small damage by breaching an obligation established by a generally binding legal regulation or an obligation imposed by a final decision of a court or arising from a contract to care for or administer another's property shall be punished by imprisonment up to two years.

(2) By imprisonment for one year to five years the offender shall be punished if they commit the act specified in paragraph 1

a) and thereby cause greater damage, or

b) from a special motive.

(3) By imprisonment for three years to ten years the offender shall be punished if they commit the act specified in paragraph 1

a) and thereby cause substantial damage, or

b) by a more serious manner of acting.

(4) By imprisonment for ten years to fifteen years the offender shall be punished if they commit the act specified in paragraph 1

a) and thereby cause damage of large extent, or

b) as a member of a dangerous grouping.

## § 238

Whoever by negligence causes another substantial damage by breaching an obligation established by a generally binding legal regulation or an obligation imposed by a final decision of a court to care for or administer another's property shall be punished by imprisonment up to two years.

## § 239

Damaging of Creditor

(1) Whoever, even if only partially, frustrates the satisfaction of their creditor by:

a) destroying, damaging, rendering unusable, concealing, selling, exchanging or otherwise removing even a part of their property,

b) encumbering a thing that is the subject of an obligation, or leasing it,

c) pretending or acknowledging a non-existent right or obligation, or assigning their claim, or assuming another's debt, even though they were not obliged or authorized to do so, or

d) pretending a reduction of their property or its extinction, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years.

(2) In the same manner as in paragraph 1, whoever, even if only partially, frustrates the satisfaction of a creditor of another person by:

a) destroying, damaging, rendering unusable, concealing, selling, exchanging, donating or otherwise removing even a part of the debtor's property, or

b) asserting a non-existent right or claim on the debtor's property, shall be punished.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years if they commit the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2

a) and thereby cause greater damage, or

b) from a special motive.

(4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one year to five years if they commit the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2 and thereby cause substantial damage.

(5) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three years to eight years if they commit the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2

a) and thereby cause damage of large extent,

b) and thereby cause insolvency to another, or

c) by a more serious manner of conduct.

## § 240

Preferential Treatment of Creditor

(1) Whoever, as a debtor who is not able to fulfil their due obligations, frustrates, even if only partially, the satisfaction of their creditor by giving preferential treatment to another creditor, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years if they cause greater damage by the act referred to in paragraph 1.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three years to eight years if they cause damage of large extent by the act referred to in paragraph 1.

## § 241

Machinations in Connection with Bankruptcy and Settlement Proceedings

(1) Whoever, as a bankruptcy creditor in connection with voting on compulsory settlement or as a creditor in connection with voting at a settlement hearing, or as a creditor in connection with voting on the approval of a restructuring plan or public preventive restructuring plan, accepts or has promised to them a pecuniary or other benefit, shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years.

(2) In the same manner as in paragraph 1, whoever provides, offers or promises a pecuniary or other benefit to a creditor in connection with voting on compulsory settlement for consent to the settlement shall be punished.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three years to eight years if they commit the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2

a) from a special motive, or

b) by a more serious manner of conduct.

Frustration of Bankruptcy or Settlement Proceedings

## § 242

(1) Whoever frustrates public preventive restructuring, bankruptcy proceedings, settlement proceedings, restructuring proceedings or debt relief proceedings by:

a) failing to fulfil an obligation imposed on them by a law that regulates such proceedings, including the obligation to file a petition for declaration of bankruptcy in time, or

b) providing false information in the list of assets and liabilities, shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to five years.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three years to ten years if they commit the act referred to in paragraph 1

a) and thereby cause greater damage,

b) from a special motive, or

c) by a more serious manner of conduct.

## § 243

(1) Whoever frustrates bankruptcy proceedings by:

a) concealing a thing belonging to the bankruptcy estate,

b) preventing a thing belonging to the bankruptcy estate from being recorded and appraised,

c) failing to release a thing belonging to the bankruptcy estate, or

d) withholding, falsifying or destroying recorded information about the debtor's property or financial activities, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to five years if they commit the act referred to in paragraph 1 and thereby cause greater damage.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three years to eight years if they commit the act referred to in paragraph 1

a) and thereby cause substantial damage, or

b) by a more serious manner of conduct.

(4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for four years to ten years if they commit the act referred to in paragraph 1 and thereby cause damage of large extent.

## § 243a

Frustration of Enforcement Proceedings

(1) Whoever frustrates enforcement proceedings by:

a) providing false information in a declaration about their property,

b) preventing a thing subject to enforcement from being recorded and appraised,

c) withholding, falsifying, altering or destroying recorded information about the obligor's property or financial activities,

d) removing, damaging or destroying a movable thing included in the inventory, or

e) failing to release a thing subject to enforcement, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years if they cause greater damage by the act referred to in paragraph 1.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three years to eight years if they cause damage of large extent by the act referred to in paragraph 1.

## § 244

Violation of Prohibition of Competition

(1) Whoever, as a person obliged to observe a prohibition of competition under law, violates the prohibition set out in such law and thereby causes greater damage to another, shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two years to eight years if they commit the act referred to in paragraph 1 and thereby cause damage of large extent.

Damaging Another's Property

## § 245

(1) Whoever destroys, damages or renders unusable another's property and thereby causes minor damage to another's property, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to one year.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years if they commit the act referred to in paragraph 1

a) and thereby cause greater damage, or

b) from a special motive.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three years to eight years if they commit the act referred to in paragraph 1

a) and thereby cause substantial damage,

b) to a thing that enjoys protection under a special regulation, or

c) by a more serious manner of conduct.

(4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for seven years to ten years if they commit the act referred to in paragraph 1

a) and thereby cause damage of large extent, or

b) as a member of a dangerous grouping.

## § 246

(1) Whoever damages another's property by spraying, painting, inscribing it with paint or another substance, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to one year.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years if they commit the act referred to in paragraph 1

a) and thereby cause greater damage, or

b) from a special motive.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three years to eight years if they commit the act referred to in paragraph 1

a) to a thing that enjoys protection under a special regulation,

b) and thereby cause substantial damage, or

c) by a more serious manner of conduct.

(4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for seven years to ten years if they commit the act referred to in paragraph 1 and thereby cause damage of large extent.

## § 247

Unauthorized Access to Computer System

(1) Whoever overcomes a security measure and thereby gains unauthorized access to a computer system or part thereof, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one year to five years if they commit the act referred to in paragraph 1

a) by a more serious manner of conduct, or

b) and thereby cause substantial damage.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three years to eight years if they commit the act referred to in paragraph 1

a) and thereby cause damage of large extent, or

b) as a member of a dangerous grouping.

## § 247a

Unauthorized Interference with Computer System

(1) Whoever restricts or interrupts the functioning of a computer system or part thereof

a) by unauthorized inputting, transmitting, damaging, deleting, degrading the quality, altering, suppressing or making inaccessible computer data, or

b) by making an unauthorized interference with the technical or software equipment of a computer and unlawfully destroys, damages, deletes, alters or reduces the quality of the information obtained, shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to eight years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1

a) and thereby causes substantial damage,

b) and thereby causes a serious disruption in the activity of a state authority, local self-government authority, court or other public authority,

c) by misusing another person's personal data in order to gain the trust of a third party, or

d) in a more serious manner of commission.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for four to ten years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1

a) and thereby causes damage on a large scale,

b) and thereby causes a serious disruption in critical infrastructure, or

c) as a member of a dangerous grouping.

## § 247b

Unauthorized Interference with Computer Data

(1) Whoever intentionally damages, deletes, alters, suppresses or makes inaccessible computer data or degrades their quality within a computer system or part thereof shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to eight years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1

a) and thereby causes substantial damage,

b) and thereby causes a serious disruption in the activity of a state authority, local self-government authority, court or other public authority,

c) by misusing another person's personal data in order to gain the trust of a third party, or

d) in a more serious manner of commission.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for four to ten years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1

a) and thereby causes damage on a large scale,

b) and thereby causes a serious disruption in critical infrastructure, or

c) as a member of a dangerous grouping.

## § 247c

Unauthorized Interception of Computer Data

(1) Whoever unlawfully intercepts computer data by technical means of non-public transmissions of computer data to, from or within a computer system, including electromagnetic emissions from a computer system containing such computer data, shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years.

(2) Whoever as an employee of an electronic communication service provider commits the act specified in paragraph 1 or intentionally enables another to commit such an act, or alters or suppresses a message submitted by means of an electronic communication service, shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to eight years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 or 2

a) from a special motive,

b) in a more serious manner of commission, or

c) thereby causes substantial damage.

(4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for four to ten years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 or 2

a) and thereby causes damage on a large scale, or

b) as a member of a dangerous grouping.

## § 247d

Production and Possession of Access Device, Computer System Password or Other Data

(1) Whoever with the intent to commit the offence of unauthorized access to a computer system under § 247, unauthorized interference with a computer system under § 247a, unauthorized interference with computer data under § 247b or unauthorized interception of computer data under § 247c produces, imports, procures, purchases, sells, exchanges, puts into circulation or makes accessible in any way

a) a device including a computer program designed for unauthorized access to a computer system or part thereof, or

b) a computer password, access code or similar data enabling access to a computer system or part thereof, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1

a) in a more serious manner of commission, or

b) and thereby causes substantial damage.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1

a) and thereby causes damage on a large scale, or

b) as a member of a dangerous grouping.

## § 248

Abuse of Ownership

(1) Whoever harms an important cultural interest or other general interest by destroying, damaging, rendering unusable or concealing his own thing or part thereof which enjoys protection under a special regulation shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 and thereby causes greater damage.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to eight years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 and thereby causes substantial damage.

(4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for four to ten years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 and thereby causes damage on a large scale or another particularly serious consequence.

## § 248a

Damaging and Degrading a Cultural Monument

(1) Whoever, even negligently, damages, degrades or destroys an immovable thing which is a cultural monument, or frustrates proceedings on the declaration of an immovable thing as a cultural monument and thereby causes substantial damage, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to one year.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1

a) in a more serious manner of commission,

b) from a special motive, or

c) thereby obtains greater benefit for himself or another.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two to six years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1

a) as a member of a dangerous grouping,

b) and thereby causes damage on a large scale, or

c) in a territory enjoying protection under a special regulation.

## § 249

Damaging and Degrading Archaeological Heritage

(1) Whoever unlawfully searches for, digs up, otherwise lifts from the site of discovery, relocates or stores an archaeological find, or otherwise damages or degrades archaeological heritage, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1

a) with a metal detector or other detection device,

b) from a special motive,

c) on a greater scale, or

d) although he was punished for a similar act in the preceding twelve months or was convicted for such an act in the preceding twenty-four months.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to eight years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1

a) on a substantial scale, or

b) in a more serious manner of commission.

(4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for five to ten years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1

a) on a large scale,

b) as a member of a dangerous grouping,

c) in a territory enjoying protection under a special regulation, or

d) in a crisis situation.

## § 249a

Counterfeiting Objects of Cultural Value

(1) Whoever unlawfully produces, imitates or alters an object of cultural value so that it is considered genuine, or procures or stores such an object for himself or another, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1

a) from a special motive,

b) on a greater scale, or

c) by means of a computer system.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to eight years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1

a) in a more serious manner of commission, or

b) on a substantial scale.

(4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for five to ten years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1

a) on a large scale,

b) as a member of a dangerous grouping, or

c) if it concerns an object of cultural value enjoying protection under a special regulation.

PIATA HLAVA

TRESTNÉ ČINY HOSPODÁRSKE

Prvý diel

Trestné činy ohrozujúce trhovú ekonomiku

## § 250

Abuse of Participation in Economic Competition

(1) Whoever abuses participation in economic competition by

a) damaging the good reputation of a competitor through unfair competition in economic relations, or

b) causing substantial damage to another competitor or endangering the operation of their business by conduct that is contrary to the law regulating the protection of economic competition, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two to six years if they commit the act specified in paragraph 1

a) and cause damage on a large scale thereby,

b) and cause the insolvency of another competitor's business thereby,

c) from a special motive, or

d) in a more serious manner of conduct.

## § 251

Unauthorized Business Activities

(1) Whoever engages in unauthorized business activities on a small scale shall be punished by imprisonment for up to one year.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years if they commit the act specified in paragraph 1

a) in a more serious manner of conduct,

b) by using persons other than as a workforce,

c) and obtain greater benefit thereby,

d) as a public official or in connection with the performance of their employment, profession or function, or

e) by providing services without professional qualifications or performing other professional activities which under the law may only be performed by a person having professional competence.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years if they commit the act specified in paragraph 1 and cause substantial damage thereby.

(4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for four to eight years if they commit the act specified in paragraph 1

a) and cause damage on a large scale thereby, or

b) as a member of a dangerous grouping.

## § 251a

Unauthorized Employment

(1) Whoever unlawfully employs a person who is staying in the territory of the Slovak Republic in violation of a generally binding legal regulation, although they have been sanctioned for a similar act in the previous twenty-four months, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years if they commit the act specified in paragraph 1 regardless of previous sanction for a similar act

a) against a protected person,

b) in a more serious manner of conduct,

c) under particularly exploitative working conditions including working conditions resulting from discrimination, when there is a striking disparity compared to the working conditions of lawfully employed persons, which affects health and safety and is contrary to human dignity, or

d) against a person who is a victim of human trafficking.

## § 251b

Fraudulent Liquidation

(1) Whoever, with the intent to thwart the resolution of the termination of business by liquidation, seeks or arranges another person who merely lends their name and surname and their identity to assume rights and obligations which they have no genuine interest in exercising, for the purpose of transferring a participation in a legal entity to such person or for the purpose of appointing such person as a statutory body or member of a statutory body of a legal entity, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to five years.

(2) The same as in paragraph 1 shall be punished whoever, with the intent to thwart the resolution of the termination of business by liquidation, transfers a participation in a legal entity to a person who merely lends their name and surname and their identity to assume rights and obligations which they have no genuine interest in exercising.

(3) The same as in paragraph 1 shall be punished whoever lends their name and surname and their identity for the purpose of transferring a participation in a legal entity or for the purpose of appointment as a statutory body or member of a statutory body of a legal entity, although they have no genuine interest in participation in the legal entity or in acting as a statutory body or member of a statutory body of the legal entity, and knew and could have known that the person who transferred the participation in the legal entity to them or appointed them as a statutory body or member of a statutory body of the legal entity, had the intent to thwart the resolution of the termination of business of the legal entity by liquidation.

(4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to ten years if they commit the act specified in paragraphs 1, 2 or 3

a) in a more serious manner of conduct, or

b) cause greater damage thereby.

(5) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for five to twelve years if they commit the act specified in paragraphs 1, 2 or 3 and cause substantial damage thereby.

(6) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for ten to fifteen years if they commit the act specified in paragraphs 1, 2 or 3

a) and cause damage on a large scale thereby, or

b) as a member of a dangerous grouping.

## § 252

Unauthorized Trading in Foreign Exchange Values and Provision of Foreign Exchange Services

(1) Whoever unlawfully trades in foreign exchange values or unlawfully provides foreign exchange monetary services

a) in a more serious manner of conduct,

b) on a larger scale, or

c) although they have been convicted for such or a similar act in the previous twenty-four months or sanctioned in the previous twelve months, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to five years if they commit the act specified in paragraph 1 and obtain substantial benefit thereby.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to eight years if they commit the act specified in paragraph 1

a) and obtain benefit on a large scale thereby,

b) as a member of a dangerous grouping, or

c) during a crisis situation.

## § 253

Unauthorized Production of Alcohol, Tobacco and Tobacco Products

(1) Whoever produces alcohol, tobacco or tobacco products without authorization in larger quantities or whoever stores or puts into circulation alcohol, tobacco or tobacco products produced without authorization in larger quantities shall be punished, unless it is an act subject to stricter punishment, by imprisonment for up to two years.

(2) The same as in paragraph 1 shall be punished whoever unlawfully manufactures or stores equipment for the production of alcohol, tobacco or tobacco products.

## § 254

Violation of Regulations on the Circulation of Goods in Relations with Foreign Countries

(1) Whoever endangers the general interest on a larger scale by violating a prohibition or restriction on the import, export or transport of goods shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years.

(2) Whoever reduces or fails to pay customs duty or other payment collected under the law upon import of goods on a larger scale shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years if they commit the act specified in paragraph 1 or 2 with at least two other persons.

(4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to eight years if they commit the act specified in paragraph 1 or 2

a) and cause substantial damage or another particularly serious consequence thereby, or

b) in a more serious manner of conduct.

(5) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for seven to twelve years if they commit the act specified in paragraph 1 or 2

a) and cause damage on a large scale thereby, or

b) as a member of a dangerous grouping.

Violation of Regulations on Handling Controlled Goods and Technologies

## § 255

(1) Whoever violates a prohibition or restriction concerning the handling of goods and technologies controlled under special regulations shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years.

(2) Whoever exports goods or technologies controlled under special regulations without authorization or transfers them to a foreign state or to an organization having its seat abroad, or to a foreign official, shall be punished by imprisonment for three to eight years.

## § 256

(1) Whoever violates or fails to fulfill an important obligation of their employment, profession, position or function and thereby causes an authorization to be unlawfully issued for handling goods and technologies controlled under special regulations or causes such goods to escape from records shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to five years if they commit the act specified in paragraph 1

a) and for that reason the goods reached a foreign country,

b) and cause substantial damage thereby, or

c) with the intent to obtain substantial benefit.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for four to eight years if they commit the act specified in paragraph 1

a) and cause damage on a large scale thereby,

b) with the intent to obtain benefit on a large scale, or

c) as a member of a dangerous grouping.

## § 257

(1) Whoever obtains, on the basis of false or incomplete information, the issuance of a document necessary for authorities controlling goods and technologies according to special regulations, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years.

(2) The same punishment as in paragraph 1 shall be imposed on whoever destroys, damages, renders unusable, conceals or fails to keep records of documents necessary for the registration of goods and technologies controlled according to special regulations, or whoever interferes with the technical or software equipment of a computer in which the registration of such goods and technologies is kept.

## § 258

Endangering Foreign Exchange Management

(1) Whoever endangers foreign exchange management by violating, on a small scale, foreign exchange regulations during the period of deposit obligation or a state of emergency in foreign exchange management, shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years.
