(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two to six years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1

a) and causes considerable damage thereby, or

b) by a more serious manner of conduct.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for four to ten years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1

a) and causes large-scale damage thereby,

b) as a member of a dangerous group, or

c) during a crisis situation.

Part Two

Criminal Offenses Against Economic Discipline

Distortion of Economic and Commercial Records Data

## § 259

(1) Whoever provides false or grossly distorted information or conceals mandatory information about material facts in a statement, report, input data entered into a computer or other documents serving for

a) statistical determination with the intention to secure unauthorized advantages for himself or another,

b) employee records with the intention to secure unauthorized advantages for himself or another,

c) accounting control,

d) control of the use of grants, subsidies or other benefits from the state budget, from the budget of a public law institution, from the budget of a state fund, from the budget of a higher territorial unit or from the budget of a municipality,

e) determination of the price of property or the rate of a security upon its transfer or passage to another person,

f) resolution of impending bankruptcy by public preventive restructuring or non-public preventive restructuring, bankruptcy, settlement, restructuring or debt relief, or

g) registration in the commercial register or land register, motor vehicle register or other register according to a special regulation, shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years.

(2) The same punishment as in paragraph 1 shall be imposed on whoever, with the intention specified in paragraph 1

a) interferes with the technical or software equipment of a computer, or

b) destroys, damages, renders unusable or fails to keep the documents specified in paragraph 1.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to eight years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 or 2

a) and causes considerable damage thereby,

b) by a more serious manner of conduct, or

c) from a special motive.

(4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for five to twelve years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 or 2

a) and causes large-scale damage thereby, or

b) and causes a particularly serious disruption to the functioning of the economy of the Slovak Republic or another particularly serious consequence.

## § 260

Whoever commits by negligence the criminal offense of distortion of economic and commercial records data by conduct specified in § 259 paragraph 1 except for letter a) or b) and causes large-scale damage thereby, shall be punished by imprisonment for three to eight years.

Damaging the Financial Interests of the European Union

## § 261

(1) Whoever uses or submits a falsified, incorrect or incomplete statement or document, or fails to provide mandatory information, and thereby enables the unlawful retention of financial resources or other assets originating from the budget of the European Union, from a budget managed by the European Union or on behalf of the European Union, or the use of these resources or assets for a purpose other than that intended, shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years.

(2) The same punishment as in paragraph 1 shall be imposed on whoever uses financial resources or other assets originating from the budget of the European Union, from a budget managed by the European Union or on behalf of the European Union for a purpose other than that intended.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one to four years if, as an employee, member, representative or other person authorized to act on behalf of the one who provides the financial resources or other assets specified in paragraph 1, he enables the acquisition of the financial resources or other assets specified in paragraph 1 by someone whom he knows does not meet the conditions specified for their provision, or enables their unlawful retention or use for a purpose other than that intended.

(4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1, 2 or 3

a) and causes greater damage thereby,

b) from a special motive, or

c) by a more serious manner of conduct.

(5) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to eight years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1, 2 or 3 and causes considerable damage thereby.

(6) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for seven to twelve years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1, 2 or 3

a) and causes large-scale damage thereby, or

b) as a member of a dangerous group.

## § 262

(1) Whoever violates or fails to fulfill an obligation arising from his employment, profession, position or function in the management or control of the activities of persons managed by him, and thereby enables the commission of a criminal offense under § 261 paragraph 1, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one to four years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 and causes considerable damage thereby.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 and causes large-scale damage thereby.

## § 263

(1) Whoever damages by negligence the financial interests of the European Union by conduct specified in § 261 paragraph 1 shall be punished by imprisonment for up to one year.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one to three years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 and causes considerable damage thereby.

## § 264

Endangering Trade, Banking, Postal, Telecommunications and Tax Secrecy

(1) Whoever spies out a trade secret, banking secret, postal secret, telecommunications secret or tax secret with the intention to reveal it to an unauthorized person, or whoever intentionally reveals such a secret to an unauthorized person, shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to eight years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1

a) and causes greater damage thereby,

b) from a special motive, or

c) by a more serious manner of conduct.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for seven to twelve years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1

a) and causes large-scale damage thereby,

b) as a member of a dangerous group, or

c) during a crisis situation.

## § 265

Misuse of Information in Business Relations

(1) Whoever unlawfully uses information not yet publicly accessible, which they obtained in their employment, profession, position or function and whose disclosure substantially influences decision-making in business relations, and carries out or gives impetus to carry out a contract or operation on an organized market for securities or commodities, or whoever unlawfully uses confidential information according to a special regulation or whoever discloses such confidential information to an unauthorized person, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to four years.

(2) In the same way as in paragraph 1, whoever, as an employee, member of a statutory body, partner, entrepreneur or participant in the business of two or more enterprises or legal entities with the same or similar subject of activity, with the intent referred to in paragraph 1, concludes or gives impetus to conclude a contract to the detriment of one or more of them, shall be punished.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to eight years if they commit the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2

a) and thereby cause greater damage,

b) from a special motive, or

c) by a more serious manner of conduct.

(4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for seven to twelve years if they commit the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2

a) and thereby cause damage on a large scale, or

b) as a member of a dangerous group.

## § 265a

Market Manipulation

(1) Whoever unlawfully

a) provides false or grossly distorting data on the supply, demand or price of a financial instrument or related spot contract relating to commodities,

b) causes the achievement or maintenance of the price of a financial instrument or related spot contract relating to commodities at an unnatural or artificial level,

c) manipulates the calculation of a benchmark value on the market, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to four years.

(2) In the same way as in paragraph 1, whoever, using fraudulent conduct or machination, carries out a transaction, gives an order to carry out a transaction, or engages in other conduct affecting the price of a financial instrument or related spot contract relating to commodities, shall be punished.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to eight years if they commit the act referred to in paragraph 1 or paragraph 2

a) and thereby cause greater damage,

b) from a special motive, or

c) by a more serious manner of conduct.

(4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for four to ten years if they commit the act referred to in paragraph 1 or paragraph 2 and thereby cause substantial damage.

(5) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for seven to twelve years if they commit the act referred to in paragraph 1 or paragraph 2

a) and thereby cause damage on a large scale, or

b) as a member of a dangerous group.

Machinations in Public Procurement and Public Auction

## § 266

(1) Whoever, in connection with public procurement or public auction, with the intent to cause damage to another or to procure benefit for themselves or another, acts in violation of a generally binding legal regulation on public procurement or public auction, or arranges for any competitor or participant in a public auction an advantage or more favorable conditions to the detriment of other competitors or participants in the public auction, shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two to eight years if they commit the act referred to in paragraph 1

a) and thereby cause substantial damage,

b) from a special motive,

c) as an announcer or organizer of a public competition or public auction, member of a privatization commission, auctioneer,

d) and demands, accepts or has promised property or other benefit, or

e) by a more serious manner of conduct.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for seven to twelve years if they commit the act referred to in paragraph 1

a) and thereby cause damage on a large scale, or

b) as a member of a dangerous group.

## § 267

Whoever engages in machinations in connection with public procurement by

a) compelling another by a more serious manner of conduct to refrain from participating in public procurement,

b) providing, offering or promising to another property or other benefit for refraining from participating in public procurement, or

c) demanding or accepting property or other benefit for refraining from participating in public procurement, shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years.

## § 268

Whoever engages in machinations in connection with a public auction of a thing by

a) compelling another by a more serious manner of conduct to refrain from participating in submitting bids at the auction,

b) providing, offering or promising to another property or other benefit for refraining from submitting bids at the public auction, or

c) demanding or accepting property or other benefit for refraining from submitting bids at the public auction, shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years.

## § 269

Harming a Consumer

(1) Whoever harms a consumer and thereby causes them minor damage by

a) deceiving the consumer as to the quality, quantity or weight of goods or as to the type, quality and quantity of performances provided,

b) placing on the market products, works or services and concealing their substantial defects, or

c) as an authorized person on the seller's side, determining a price offer on the basis of which goods are sold or services provided at a price exceeding the limit established by a generally binding legal regulation or by a decision issued on its basis, shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to eight years if they commit the act referred to in paragraph 1

a) and thereby obtain for themselves or another greater benefit,

b) against a protected person,

c) by a more serious manner of conduct, or

d) although they were convicted for such an act in the preceding twenty-four months or released from serving a sentence of imprisonment imposed for such an act.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for four to ten years if they commit the act referred to in paragraph 1 and thereby obtain for themselves or another benefit on a substantial scale or cause disruption in supply.

(4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for seven to twelve years if they commit the act referred to in paragraph 1 and thereby obtain for themselves or another benefit on a large scale.

## § 269a

Unfair Commercial Practices towards Consumers

(1) Whoever violates consumer rights by conduct consisting of commercial practices which, according to a special regulation on consumer protection, are considered unfair in all circumstances,

a) although they were convicted for such an act in the preceding twenty-four months or released from serving a sentence of imprisonment imposed for such an act, or

b) although they were penalized for a similar act in the preceding twenty-four months, shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years.

(2) In the same way as in paragraph 1, whoever violates consumer rights by giving an instruction for conduct consisting of commercial practices according to paragraph 1, although the seller was penalized for similar conduct in the preceding twenty-four months, shall be punished.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to eight years if they commit the act referred to in paragraph 1 or paragraph 2 against a protected person.

Third Part

Offences against Currency and Tax Offences

## § 270

Counterfeiting, Alteration and Unauthorized Production of Money and Securities

(1) Whoever procures for himself or another counterfeit, altered or unauthorizedly produced money or securities or whoever keeps such money or securities, shall be punished by imprisonment for three to eight years.

(2) Whoever counterfeits, alters or unauthorizedly produces money or securities or whoever counterfeits, alters or unauthorizedly produces money or securities with the intent to pass them off as genuine or as money or securities of higher value, or whoever passes off counterfeit, altered or unauthorizedly produced money or securities as genuine, shall be punished by imprisonment for seven to ten years.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for ten to fifteen years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2

a) by a more serious manner of commission, or

b) on a larger scale.

(4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for twelve to twenty years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2

a) as a member of a dangerous grouping, or

b) on a large scale.

## § 271

Putting into Circulation Counterfeit, Altered and Unauthorizedly Produced Money and Securities

(1) Whoever imports, exports, transports, receives or obtains counterfeit, altered and unauthorizedly produced money and securities with the purpose of putting them into circulation, shall be punished by imprisonment for seven to ten years.

(2) Whoever puts into circulation as genuine counterfeit or altered, or unauthorizedly produced money with which he was paid as genuine, shall be punished by imprisonment up to two years.

(3) In the same manner as in paragraph 2 shall be punished whoever uses counterfeit or altered, or unauthorizedly produced securities as genuine.

## § 272

Production and Possession of Counterfeiting Equipment

(1) Whoever produces, procures for himself or another or keeps a tool or other object, or computer program intended for counterfeiting or altering money or its protective elements, securities, public documents, official seals and official closures or marks, shall be punished by imprisonment up to three years.

(2) In the same manner as in paragraph 1 shall be punished whoever unauthorizedly using equipment or material intended for the production of money produces, receives, obtains or keeps holograms, watermarks or other protective elements of money which serve to protect against their counterfeiting.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2 in the exercise of his profession.

## § 273

Endangering the Circulation of Money

Whoever without lawful reason refuses domestic money or damages domestic money shall be punished by imprisonment up to six months.

## § 274

Counterfeiting, Alteration and Unauthorized Production of Revenue Stamps, Postal Items, Stickers and Postal Stamps

(1) Whoever counterfeits, alters or unauthorizedly produces revenue stamps, domestic or foreign postal items including items withdrawn from circulation, postal stickers or postal stamps with the intent to cause damage to another or procure unauthorized benefit for himself or another, or whoever puts such stamps, items, stickers or stamps into circulation or uses them as genuine, shall be punished by imprisonment up to one year.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to five years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1

a) and obtains thereby a larger benefit,

b) from a special motive, or

c) by a more serious manner of commission.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for four to eight years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1

a) and obtains thereby benefit on a large scale, or

b) as a member of a dangerous grouping.

## § 275

Counterfeiting and Alteration of Control Technical Measures for Marking Goods

(1) Whoever counterfeits or alters control stamps, control strips or other control technical measures for marking goods for tax purposes or for other purposes established by a generally binding legal regulation with the intent to cause damage to another or procure unauthorized benefit for himself or another or whoever puts such control stamps into circulation or uses them as genuine, or keeps them, shall be punished by imprisonment up to one year.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to five years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1

a) and obtains thereby a larger benefit,

b) from a special motive, or

c) by a more serious manner of commission.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for four to eight years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1

a) and obtains thereby benefit on a large scale, or

b) as a member of a dangerous grouping.

## § 276

Reduction of Tax and Insurance Premiums

(1) Whoever on a small scale reduces tax, social insurance premiums, public health insurance or contribution to old-age pension savings, shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to eight years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1

a) and has already been convicted of such an act,

b) and to facilitate the commission of such an act violates an official closure,

c) by a more serious manner of commission, or

d) on a larger scale.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for four to ten years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 on a substantial scale.

(4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for seven to twelve years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 on a large scale.

## § 277

Non-Remittance of Tax and Insurance Premiums

(1) Whoever on a small scale withholds and fails to remit to the designated recipient payable tax, social insurance premiums, public health insurance or contribution to old-age pension savings, which he deducts or collects according to law, with the intent to procure unauthorized benefit for himself or another, shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to eight years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1

a) by a more serious manner of commission, or

b) on a larger scale.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for four to ten years if by the act referred to in paragraph 1 he causes substantial damage.

(4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for seven to twelve years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 on a large scale.

## § 277a

Tax Fraud

(1) Whoever unauthorizedly on a larger scale claims a refund of value added tax or excise duty with the intent to procure unauthorized benefit for himself or another, shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for four to ten years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1

a) and has already been convicted of such an act,

b) on a substantial scale, or

c) by a more serious manner of commission.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for seven to twelve years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1

a) on a large scale, or

b) as a member of a dangerous grouping.

## § 278

Non-Payment of Tax and Insurance Premiums

(1) Whoever on a larger scale fails to pay payable tax, social insurance premiums, public health insurance or contribution to old-age pension savings, shall be punished by imprisonment up to three years.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 on a substantial scale.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to eight years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 on a large scale.

## § 278a

Obstruction of Tax Administration

(1) Whoever obstructs the performance of tax administration by

a) stating false or grossly distorted data in documents submitted in tax administration, or concealing mandatory data on facts decisive for the correct determination of tax,

b) altering, devaluing or destroying documents decisive for the correct determination of tax,

c) failing to fulfill the notification obligation imposed on him by law, or

d) failing to fulfill the obligation imposed on him by law during a tax audit, although he was punished for a similar act in the previous twelve months, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1

a) from a special motive, or

b) in a more serious manner of commission.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to eight years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 as a member of a dangerous grouping.

## § 279

Violation of Regulations on State Technical Measures for Marking Goods

(1) Whoever handles control stamps, control strips or other control technical measures for marking goods for tax purposes or for other purposes established by law in violation of a generally binding legal regulation with the intention of causing damage to another or procuring for himself or another an unauthorized benefit, or whoever, in violation of a generally binding legal regulation, imports, exports, transports, has transported, puts into circulation or stores goods without control stamps, control strips or without other control technical measures for marking them for tax purposes or for other purposes established by law, shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1

a) and thereby causes greater damage,

b) from a special motive, or

c) in a more serious manner of commission.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to eight years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1

a) and thereby causes damage on a large scale, or

b) as a member of a dangerous grouping.

## § 280

Common Provision

Protection under this part is provided, in addition to valid money, also to invalid money during the period designated for its exchange, money not issued at the time of commission of the criminal offense but designated for issue into circulation, as well as foreign money including the euro currency and foreign securities including foreign securities issued en masse, which are payable to bearer or are transferable by endorsement.

Fourth Part

Criminal Offenses Against Industrial Rights and Against Copyright

## § 281

Violation of Rights to Trademark, Indication of Origin of Product and Trade Name

(1) Whoever puts into circulation goods or provides services unlawfully marked with a designation identical or confusable with a trademark, the right to use which belongs to another, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years.

(2) In the same manner as in paragraph 1 shall be punished whoever, to achieve economic benefit,

a) puts into circulation goods unlawfully marked with a designation identical or confusable with a registered indication of origin of product and geographical indication of product, the right to use which belongs to another, or

b) unlawfully uses a designation identical or confusable with a trade name or name of a legal person or natural person.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 or 2

a) and thereby causes substantial damage,

b) from a special motive, or

c) in a more serious manner of commission.

(4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to eight years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 or 2

a) and thereby causes damage on a large scale, or

b) as a member of a dangerous grouping.

## § 282

Violation of Industrial Rights

(1) Whoever unlawfully interferes with rights to a patent, utility model, design, topography of a semiconductor product or to recognized plant varieties or animal breeds shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1

a) and thereby causes substantial damage, or

b) in a more serious manner of commission.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to eight years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1

a) and thereby causes damage on a large scale, or

b) as a member of a dangerous grouping.

## § 283

Violation of Copyright

(1) Whoever unlawfully interferes with legally protected rights to a work, artistic performance, sound recording or audiovisual recording, radio broadcasting or television broadcasting or database shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1

a) and thereby causes greater damage,

b) in a more serious manner of commission,

c) from a special motive, or

d) through a computer system.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 and thereby causes substantial damage.

(4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to eight years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1

a) and thereby causes damage on a large scale, or

b) as a member of a dangerous grouping.

SIXTH CHAPTER

GENERALLY DANGEROUS CRIMINAL OFFENSES AND OFFENSES AGAINST THE ENVIRONMENT

First Part

Generally Dangerous Criminal Offenses

General Endangerment

## § 284

(1) Whoever intentionally

a) exposes people to danger of death or serious injury to health or the property of another to danger of damage on a large scale by causing fire or flood, or a defect or accident of a means of mass transport, or a harmful effect of explosives, gas, electricity, radioactivity or other similarly dangerous substances or forces, or commits another similar dangerous act (general danger), or

b) increases general danger or makes its aversion or mitigation more difficult, shall be punished by imprisonment for four to ten years.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for ten to fifteen years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1

a) in a more serious manner of commission,

b) on a protected person,

c) from a special motive, or

d) in order to frustrate or make more difficult for another the exercise of his fundamental rights and freedoms.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for fifteen to twenty years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1

a) and thereby causes serious injury to health or death, or

b) as a member of a dangerous grouping.

(4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for twenty to twenty-five years or by life imprisonment if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1

a) and thereby causes serious injury to health to several persons or death of several persons, or

b) in a crisis situation.

## § 285

(1) Whoever by negligence causes or increases general danger, or makes its aversion or mitigation more difficult, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to one year.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 in a more serious manner of commission.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two to five years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 and thereby causes

a) substantial damage, or

b) serious injury to health or death.

(4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for four to ten years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 and thereby causes serious injury to health to several persons or death of several persons.

Damaging and Endangering the Operation of a Generally Beneficial Facility

## § 286

(1) Whoever intentionally endangers the operation of

a) a public telecommunications facility, public postal network, or means of mass transport, or removes or renders unusable a vertical traffic sign prohibitory or mandatory,

b) a protective device against the leakage of pollutants,

c) an energy facility or public water supply, or public sewerage,

d) a public protective device against fire, flood, or other emergency event,

e) a submarine cable or submarine pipeline,

f) a defence or protective device against aerial and other similar attacks or their consequences,

g) structures and facilities serving forestry, or

h) a similar generally beneficial facility, shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years.

(2) By imprisonment for four to eight years the offender shall be punished if he commits the act specified in subsection 1

a) and thereby causes disruption of the operation of a generally beneficial facility, or

b) during a crisis situation.

## § 287

Whoever intentionally destroys or renders unusable a clearly marked marker of a point of the astronomic-geodetic network or a fundamental levelling point, or a reference gravimetric point, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to one year.

## § 288

Whoever through negligence endangers the operation of a generally beneficial facility pursuant to § 286, with the exception of a vertical traffic sign prohibitory or mandatory, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to six months.

## § 289

Endangerment under the influence of an addictive substance

(1) Whoever performs, in a state excluding capability, which he caused by the influence of an addictive substance, employment or other activity in which he could endanger the life or health of persons or cause considerable damage to property, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to one year.

(2) In the same manner as in subsection 1 shall be punished whoever, in the performance of employment or other activity specified in subsection 1, refuses to undergo examination for the detection of an addictive substance, which is carried out by a breath test or an orientation testing device, or refuses to undergo medical examination by collection and examination of blood or other biological material, as to whether he is influenced by an addictive substance, even though such examination would not be associated with danger to his health.

(3) Whoever performs, in a state excluding capability, which he caused by the influence of an addictive substance, employment or other activity in which he could endanger the life or health of persons or cause considerable damage to property,

a) even though he was convicted for such an act or for the act specified in subsection 2 in the preceding twenty-four months, or released from the execution of a sentence of imprisonment imposed for such an act or for the act specified in subsection 2,

b) even though he was penalised for a similar act committed under the influence of an addictive substance or for the act specified in subsection 2 in the preceding twenty-four months, or

c) caused, even through negligence, injury to the health of another or greater damage to the property of another, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years.

(4) By imprisonment for two to five years the offender shall be punished if he performs, in a state excluding capability, which he caused by the influence of an addictive substance, conduct in which he could endanger the life or health of persons or cause considerable damage to property in the performance of employment or other activity in which the influence of an addictive substance is particularly dangerous, especially if he operates a means of mass transport.

## § 290

Violation of obligations in imminent distress

(1) Whoever frustrates or hampers the averting or mitigation of imminent distress that directly affects a group of persons by, without serious reason,

a) refusing assistance that is imposed on him by law or to which he has committed, or

b) frustrating the provision of such assistance by another person, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years.

(2) By imprisonment for six months to five years the offender shall be punished if he commits the act specified in subsection 1

a) in a more serious manner of conduct,

b) against a protected person,

c) from a special motive, or

d) notwithstanding that he himself caused the imminent distress.

(3) By imprisonment for three to eight years the offender shall be punished if he commits the act specified in subsection 1

a) and thereby causes serious harm to health or death, or

b) during a crisis situation.

## § 290a

Violation of obligations during a crisis situation

(1) Whoever, during a crisis situation, refuses to perform or intentionally fails to perform an obligation imposed by public authorities for the defence of the State and the preservation of its security, for the protection of the life and health of persons, for the protection of property, for the observance of fundamental rights and freedoms, for the averting of danger, or for the restoration of disrupted economy, in particular the proper functioning of supply, transport, and public services in municipalities and the proper functioning of constitutional bodies, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years.

(2) By imprisonment for two to five years the offender shall be punished if he commits the act specified in subsection 1

a) and thereby causes serious harm to health or death, or

b) in a more serious manner of conduct.

## § 290b

Evading the performance of obligations during a crisis situation

(1) Whoever, during a crisis situation, with the intention of evading an obligation imposed by public authorities for the defence of the State and the preservation of its security, for the protection of the life and health of persons, for the protection of property, for the observance of fundamental rights and freedoms, for the averting of danger, or for the restoration of disrupted economy, in particular the proper functioning of supply, transport, and public services in municipalities and the proper functioning of constitutional bodies, injures himself, feigns illness, forges a document, abuses an addictive substance, or uses another ruse, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years.

(2) By imprisonment for two to five years the offender shall be punished if he commits the act specified in subsection 1

a) and thereby causes serious harm to health or death, or

b) in a more serious manner of conduct.

Endangerment of the safety of an aircraft and a ship

## § 291

(1) Whoever, on board an aircraft or a ship, with the intention of obtaining or exercising control over such means,

a) uses violence against another or the threat of immediate violence,

b) threatens another with killing, injury to health, or causing damage on a large scale, or

c) exploits the defencelessness of another, shall be punished by imprisonment for ten to fifteen years.

(2) By imprisonment for fifteen to twenty years the offender shall be punished if he commits the act specified in subsection 1

a) and thereby causes serious harm to health or death, or

b) in a more serious manner of conduct.

(3) By imprisonment for twenty to twenty-five years or by a sentence of life imprisonment the offender shall be punished if he commits the act specified in subsection 1

a) and thereby causes serious harm to health of multiple persons or death of multiple persons,

b) as a member of a dangerous grouping, or

c) during a crisis situation.

## § 292

Whoever communicates false information that may endanger the security or operation of an aircraft in flight or a ship at sea, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years.

## § 293

Diversion of an aircraft to a foreign country

(1) Whoever, for the purpose of diverting an aircraft to a foreign country, takes control of such means or unlawfully uses such means that has been entrusted to him, shall be punished by imprisonment for seven to fifteen years.

(2) By imprisonment for fifteen to twenty-five years or by a sentence of life imprisonment the offender shall be punished if he causes death by the act specified in subsection 1.

Unlawful arming and trading in weapons

## § 294

(1) Whoever manufactures, imports, exports, transits, transports, procures or possesses ammunition for himself or another without authorization, or brokers such activity, shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years.

(2) Whoever manufactures, imports, exports, transports, procures or possesses a firearm, its part or component for himself or another without authorization or without marking of the weapon designated for its identification according to an international treaty by which the Slovak Republic is bound, or brokers such activity, shall be punished by imprisonment for three to eight years.

(3) In the same manner as in paragraph 2, the offender shall be punished if he falsifies, illicitly obliterates, removes or otherwise alters the marking designated for its identification and tracing on a firearm according to an international treaty by which the Slovak Republic is bound.

(4) By imprisonment for four to ten years, the offender shall be punished if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1, 2 or 3

a) by a more serious manner of commission,

b) from a special motive, or

c) to a larger extent.

(5) By imprisonment for eight to fifteen years, the offender shall be punished if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1, 2 or 3

a) as a member of a dangerous grouping,

b) to a large extent, or

c) during a crisis situation.

## § 295

(1) Whoever without authorization

a) manufactures, imports, exports, transits, transports, procures or keeps for himself or another a weapon of mass destruction or its part or component,

b) accumulates firearms, weapons of mass destruction, ammunition or explosives, or

c) brokers any of the activities specified in letter a) or b), shall be punished by imprisonment for three to eight years.

(2) In the same manner as in paragraph 1, the offender shall be punished if he

a) develops, manufactures, imports, exports, transits, transports, procures, possesses, stores or uses an anti-personnel mine for himself or another, or

b) designs a facility or operates an installation for the production of chemical weapons or biological weapons.

(3) By imprisonment for seven to ten years, the offender shall be punished if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 or 2

a) by a more serious manner of commission,

b) from a special motive, or

c) to a larger extent.

(4) By imprisonment for ten to fifteen years, the offender shall be punished if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 or 2

a) as a member of a dangerous grouping, or

b) to a substantial extent.

(5) By imprisonment for fifteen to twenty years, the offender shall be punished if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 or 2

a) to a large extent, or

b) during a crisis situation.

## § 296

Establishment, Organizing and Supporting a Criminal Group

Whoever establishes or organizes a criminal group, is a member thereof, is active for it or supports it, shall be punished by imprisonment for five to ten years.

## § 297

Establishment, Organizing and Supporting a Terrorist Group

Whoever establishes or organizes a terrorist group, is a member thereof, is active for it or supports it, shall be punished by imprisonment for ten to fifteen years.

Unlawful Production and Possession of Nuclear Materials, Radioactive Substances, High-Risk Chemical Substances and High-Risk Biological Agents and Toxins

## § 298

(1) Whoever, even through negligence, without authorization manufactures, imports, exports, transits, buys, sells, offers, exchanges, modifies, uses, delivers for transport, deposits, removes or otherwise procures or keeps for himself or another nuclear or other radioactive material or a high-risk chemical substance, or a high-risk biological agent or toxin, or items designated for their production, shall be punished by imprisonment for one to six years.

(2) By imprisonment for three to ten years, the offender shall be punished if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1

a) by a more serious manner of commission, or

b) from a special motive.

(3) By imprisonment for ten to twenty years, the offender shall be punished if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1

a) and thereby causes serious bodily harm or death,

b) and thereby obtains substantial benefit, or

c) as a member of a dangerous grouping.

(4) By imprisonment for twenty to twenty-five years or by a sentence of life imprisonment, the offender shall be punished if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1

a) and thereby causes serious bodily harm to multiple persons or death of multiple persons,

b) and thereby obtains benefit to a large extent, or

c) during a crisis situation.

## § 299

(1) Whoever manufactures, procures or keeps for himself or another an item designated for the unlawful production of nuclear or other radioactive substance, or a high-risk chemical substance, or a high-risk biological agent and toxin, shall be punished by imprisonment for one to six years.

(2) By imprisonment for three to eight years, the offender shall be punished if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 and thereby obtains greater benefit for himself or another.

(3) By imprisonment for four to ten years, the offender shall be punished if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 and thereby obtains substantial benefit for himself or another.

(4) By imprisonment for ten to fifteen years, the offender shall be punished if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 and thereby obtains benefit to a large extent for himself or another.

## § 299a

Unauthorized Execution of Construction

(1) Whoever, without a construction permit or in violation thereof, erects a structure or its part, where it is not a simple structure or minor structure according to construction regulations, and thereby causes serious harm to the rights or legitimate interests of the landowner or multiple persons, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years.

(2) By imprisonment for three to five years, the offender shall be punished if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1, even though he was convicted of such or a similar act in the preceding twenty-four months or was penalized in the preceding twenty-four months.

Part Two

Criminal Offences against the Environment Endangerment and Damage to the Environment

## § 300

(1) Whoever intentionally endangers the environment with the risk of minor damage by violating generally binding legal regulations on the protection of the environment or on the protection of natural resources including natural healing resources and natural resources of mineral table waters and their management, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years.

(2) Whoever violates generally binding legal regulations on the protection of the environment or on the protection of natural resources including natural healing resources and natural resources of mineral table waters and their management and thereby endangers another with the risk of serious bodily harm or death, shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years.

(3) In the same manner as in paragraph 2, the offender shall be punished if he unlawfully erects a structure in a protected area.

(4) In the same manner as in paragraph 2, the offender shall be punished if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1

a) by a more serious manner of commission, or

b) in a protected area including the protective zone of natural healing resources and natural resources of mineral table waters.

(5) By imprisonment for three to eight years, the offender shall be punished if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 or paragraph 2 and thereby causes substantial damage to the environment.

(6) By imprisonment for four to ten years, the offender shall be punished if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 or paragraph 2

a) and thereby causes serious bodily harm or death, or

b) and thereby causes damage to a large extent.

## § 301

(1) Whoever by negligence endangers the environment with the risk of greater damage by violating generally binding legal regulations on the protection of the environment or on the protection of natural resources including natural healing resources and natural resources of mineral table waters and their management, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to one year.

(2) Whoever by negligence violates generally binding legal regulations on the protection of the environment or on the protection of natural resources including natural healing resources and natural resources of mineral table waters and their management and thereby endangers another with serious injury to health or death, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years.

(3) In the same manner as in paragraph 2, the offender shall be punished if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 in a protected area including the protection zone of natural healing resources and natural resources of mineral table waters.

(4) By imprisonment for one year to five years the offender shall be punished if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 or paragraph 2 and causes thereby considerable damage.

(5) By imprisonment for three years to eight years the offender shall be punished if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 or paragraph 2

a) and causes thereby serious injury to health or death, or

b) and causes thereby damage of large scale.

## § 302

Unauthorized Handling of Waste

(1) Whoever, even by negligence, handles waste on a small scale in violation of generally binding legal regulations, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years.

(2) By imprisonment for six months to three years the offender shall be punished if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1

a) and endangers the environment with the risk of greater damage, or

b) and by such act endangers another with serious injury to health or death.

(3) By imprisonment for one year to five years the offender shall be punished if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 on a considerable scale.

(4) By imprisonment for three years to eight years the offender shall be punished if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1

a) and causes thereby serious injury to health or death, or

b) on a large scale.

## § 302a

Unauthorized Discharge of Polluting Substances

(1) Whoever in violation of generally binding legal regulations or an international treaty, even by negligence, discharges, or despite the fact that such obligation arises from his employment, profession, position or function, fails to prevent the discharge of an oil substance, harmful liquid substance or other polluting substance from a seagoing vessel or seagoing recreational craft,

a) and thereby causes greater damage, or

b) repeatedly, shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years.

(2) By imprisonment for one year to five years the offender shall be punished if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1

a) by a more serious manner of conduct

b) and causes thereby damage of considerable scale, or

c) although he has already been convicted of such an act in the preceding twenty-four months or has been punished for a similar act in the preceding twenty-four months.

(3) By imprisonment for three years to eight years the offender shall be punished if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1

a) and causes thereby serious injury to health or death,

b) and causes thereby damage of large scale, or

c) as a member of a dangerous grouping.

Violation of Water and Air Protection

## § 303

(1) Whoever acts in violation of generally binding legal regulations on the protection of waters and air and causes deterioration of the quality of surface waters or groundwaters, or air such that

a) he endangers another with serious injury to health or death, or

b) causes the risk of considerable damage, shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years.

(2) By imprisonment for one year to five years the offender shall be punished if by the act specified in paragraph 1 he causes considerable damage.

(3) By imprisonment for three years to eight years the offender shall be punished if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1

a) and causes thereby serious injury to health or death, or

b) and causes thereby damage of large scale.

## § 304

(1) Whoever by negligence acts in violation of generally binding legal regulations on the protection of waters or air and causes emergency deterioration of the quality of surface waters or groundwaters, or air such that

a) he endangers another with serious injury to health or death, or

b) causes the risk of considerable damage, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to one year.

(2) By imprisonment for six months to three years the offender shall be punished if by the act specified in paragraph 1 he causes considerable damage.

(3) By imprisonment for one year to five years the offender shall be punished if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1

a) and causes thereby serious injury to health or death, or

b) and causes thereby damage of large scale.

## § 304a

Unauthorized Production and Handling of Ozone-Depleting Substances

(1) Whoever, even by negligence, in violation of generally binding legal regulations produces, imports, exports, places in circulation or otherwise unlawfully uses an ozone-depleting substance, shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years.

(2) By imprisonment for one year to five years the offender shall be punished if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 and causes thereby considerable damage.

(3) By imprisonment for three years to eight years the offender shall be punished if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 and causes thereby damage of large scale.

## § 305

Violation of Plant and Animal Protection

(1) Whoever, even by negligence, in violation of generally binding legal regulations on the protection of nature and landscape or generally binding legal regulations on the protection of specimens by regulation of trade in them on a larger scale

a) damages, destroys, uproots, digs up or collects a protected plant or damages or destroys its biotope,

b) kills, injures, captures or relocates a protected animal or damages or destroys its biotope and dwelling,

c) damages or destroys a natural biotope, or

d) endangers a protected animal species or plant species, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years.

(2) Whoever in violation of generally binding regulations on the protection of nature and landscape or generally binding regulations in the field of forestry unlawfully drives a motor vehicle, motor tricycle, motor quad, motorcycle or scooter on forest or agricultural land, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to one year.

(3) Whoever, even by negligence, in violation of generally binding legal regulations on the protection of nature and landscape or generally binding legal regulations on the protection of specimens by regulation of trade in them on a larger scale

a) obtains for himself or procures for another a protected animal or protected plant, or specimen,

b) keeps, grows, breeds, processes, imports or exports protected plants or protected animals, or specimens or trades in them, or otherwise alienates them, or

c) deliberately removes, falsifies, alters or otherwise unlawfully uses the unique identification of protected animals or specimens, shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years.

(4) By imprisonment for one year to five years the offender shall be punished if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1, 2 or 3

a) by a more serious manner of conduct,

b) from a special motive,

c) on a considerable scale,

d) with the intent to obtain for himself or another considerable benefit, or

e) although he was convicted of such an act in the preceding twenty-four months or was punished for a similar act in the preceding twenty-four months.

(5) By imprisonment for three years to eight years the offender shall be punished if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1, 2 or 3

a) as a member of a dangerous grouping,

b) on a large scale, or

c) with the intent to procure for himself or another benefit of large scale.

## § 305a

Animal cruelty

(1) Whoever

a) tortures an animal, although they were punished for a similar act in the preceding twelve months or convicted of such an act in the preceding twenty-four months,

b) tortures an animal in a particularly cruel and brutal manner, or

c) tortures an animal to death, shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one year to five years if they commit the act specified in paragraph 1

a) on multiple animals,

b) publicly or in a place accessible to the public,

c) on an animal specially protected by law or on an animal used for therapeutic, sports or recreational purposes, or

d) in a more serious manner of conduct.

## § 305aa

Killing a companion animal without reasonable cause

(1) Whoever without reasonable cause established by a special regulation kills a companion animal or produces from a killed companion animal a product of animal origin or animal by-product, trades in it or otherwise alienates it, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one year to five years if they commit the act specified in paragraph 1

a) on multiple animals,

b) publicly or in a place accessible to the public,

c) from a special motive, or

d) although they were convicted of such an act in the preceding twenty-four months.

## § 305b

Neglect of care for an animal

(1) Whoever negligently causes death or permanent consequences to the health of an animal which they own or are obliged to care for by neglecting the necessary care for this animal, although they were punished for a similar act in the preceding twenty-four months, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one year to five years if they commit the act specified in paragraph 1 regardless of whether they were punished for a similar act in the preceding twenty-four months,

a) on multiple animals,

b) publicly or in a place accessible to the public,

c) on an animal specially protected by law, or

d) in a more serious manner of conduct.

## § 305c

Organizing animal fights

(1) Whoever

a) organizes a fight between animals,

b) for the purpose according to letter a) incites an animal against an animal, trains an animal on another animal, breeds, keeps, trades or otherwise deals with animals or enables such conduct, or

c) participates in an organized animal fight, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one year to five years if they commit the act specified in paragraph 1

a) publicly or in a place accessible to the public,

b) on an animal specially protected by law,

c) in a more serious manner of conduct, or

d) in the presence of a group of persons younger than eighteen years.

## § 305d

Common provision

(1) For the purposes of § 305a to 305c, an animal means a dog, cat, equine animal, farm animal according to a special regulation and protected animal according to a special regulation kept in captivity.

(2) For the purposes of § 305aa, a companion animal means a dog, cat or ferret.

## § 306

Violation of protection of trees and shrubs

(1) Whoever in violation of generally binding legal regulations in the field of forestry or generally binding legal regulations on the protection of nature and landscape damages or destroys a tree or shrub on a larger scale, or cuts them down, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one year to five years if they commit the act specified in paragraph 1

a) on a considerable scale, or

b) although they were convicted of such an act in the preceding twenty-four months or were punished for a similar act in the preceding twenty-four months.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three years to eight years if they commit the act specified in paragraph 1

a) as a member of a dangerous grouping, or

b) on a large scale.

Spreading infectious disease of animals and plants

## § 307

(1) Whoever, even through negligence, causes danger of introduction or spread of an infectious disease of domestic or economically important animals, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to one year.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years if they commit the act specified in paragraph 1

a) and thereby causes greater damage,

b) and thereby causes the spread of such disease, or

c) in a more serious manner of conduct.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one year to five years if they commit the act specified in paragraph 1 and thereby cause considerable damage.

## § 308

(1) Whoever, even through negligence, causes danger of introduction or spread of an infectious disease or pest of useful plants, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to one year.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years if they commit the act specified in paragraph 1

a) and causes the spread of such disease or pest, or

b) in a more serious manner of conduct.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one year to five years if they commit the act specified in paragraph 1 and thereby cause considerable damage.

## § 309

Escape of organisms

(1) Whoever in violation of generally binding legal regulations on the use of genetic technologies causes the escape of genetically modified organisms from enclosed spaces or causes the introduction of genetically modified organisms into the environment, which may endanger people or the environment, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one year to five years if they commit the act specified in paragraph 1

a) and thereby causes greater damage,

b) from a special motive, or

c) in a more serious manner of conduct.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for four years to ten years if they commit the act specified in paragraph 1

a) and thereby causes damage to the environment on a considerable scale, or

b) and thereby causes serious harm to health or death.

(4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for ten years to twenty years if they commit the act specified in paragraph 1

a) and thereby causes serious harm to health of multiple persons or death of multiple persons, or

b) during a crisis situation.

## § 310

Poaching

(1) Whoever unlawfully interferes with the exercise of hunting rights or the exercise of fishing rights by hunting game or fish without permission or hunting game or fish during their protection period or in a prohibited manner, or whoever conceals, keeps, or transfers to themselves or another game or fish unlawfully caught or found, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years if they commit the act specified in paragraph 1

a) in a mass-effective or reprehensible manner,

b) on a small scale, or

c) as a person who has a specially imposed obligation to protect the environment.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one year to five years if they commit the act specified in paragraph 1

a) and was already convicted of such an act,

b) on a larger scale, or

c) in a more serious manner of conduct.

(4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three years to eight years if they commit the act specified in paragraph 1 on a considerable scale.

(5) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for four years to ten years if they commit the act specified in paragraph 1 on a large scale.

SEVENTH CHAPTER

CRIMINAL OFFENCES AGAINST THE REPUBLIC

First Part

Criminal offences against the foundations of the republic

## § 311

High treason

A citizen of the Slovak Republic who, in connection with a foreign power or a foreign agent, commits the criminal offence of conspiracy against the Slovak Republic, terror, harmful activity or sabotage, shall be punished by imprisonment for fifteen years to twenty-five years or by life imprisonment.

## § 312

Conspiracy against the Slovak Republic

(1) Whoever attempts by violence or threat of violence

a) to change the constitutional order, violate the independence or sovereignty of the Slovak Republic, or

b) to violate the territorial integrity of the Slovak Republic, shall be punished by imprisonment for ten to twelve years.

(2) Whoever commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 shall be punished by imprisonment for fifteen to twenty-five years or by life imprisonment

a) and thereby causes the death of multiple persons,

b) and thereby causes damage on a large scale or another particularly serious consequence,

c) as a member of a dangerous grouping,

d) as a public official, or

e) during a crisis situation.

Terror

## § 313

Whoever, with the intent to damage the constitutional order of the Slovak Republic, intentionally kills another person or attempts to do so, shall be punished by imprisonment for twenty to twenty-five years or by life imprisonment.

## § 314

(1) Whoever takes a hostage and threatens to kill him or to cause him harm to health or other harm with the aim of forcing the fulfillment of conditions damaging the constitutional order of the Slovak Republic, shall be punished by imprisonment for seven to twelve years.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for twelve to twenty years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1

a) and thereby causes serious harm to health or death,

b) on a protected person, or

c) by a more serious manner of conduct.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for fifteen to twenty-five years or by life imprisonment if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1

a) and thereby causes the death of multiple persons,

b) as a member of a dangerous grouping, or

c) during a crisis situation.

Wrecking

## § 315

(1) Whoever, with the intent to damage the constitutional order or defense capability of the Slovak Republic

a) exposes a group of persons to danger of death, serious harm to health or property of another to danger of damage on a large scale by causing fire, flood or malfunction, or accident of a means of mass transport, or harmful effect of explosives, gas, electricity, radioactivity or other similarly dangerous substances or forces, or increases the danger, or makes its prevention or mitigation more difficult, or

b) commits another similar dangerous act, shall be punished by imprisonment for seven to twelve years.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for twelve to twenty years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1

a) and thereby causes greater damage,

b) by a more serious manner of conduct, or

c) on a protected person.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for fifteen to twenty-five years or by life imprisonment if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1

a) and thereby causes serious harm to health or death,

b) and thereby causes damage on a large scale or another particularly serious consequence, or

c) as a member of a dangerous grouping.

(4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for twenty to twenty-five years or by life imprisonment if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1

a) and thereby causes serious harm to health to multiple persons or death of multiple persons, or

b) during a crisis situation.

## § 316

(1) Whoever, with the intent referred to in § 315 paragraph 1, destroys, damages or renders unusable property of another or his own property, shall be punished by imprisonment for four to ten years.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for ten to twenty-five years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 during a crisis situation.

## § 317

Sabotage

(1) Whoever, with the intent to damage the constitutional order or defense capability of the Slovak Republic, abuses his employment, profession, position or his function or commits another act in order to

a) obstruct or make more difficult the fulfillment of an important task of a state authority, armed forces or armed corps, legal entity, or

b) cause a malfunction or other serious damage in the activity of such authority or such organization or institution, shall be punished by imprisonment for four to ten years.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for ten to fifteen years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1

a) and thereby causes serious harm to health or death,

b) and thereby causes greater damage, or

c) by a more serious manner of conduct.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for fifteen to twenty-five years or by life imprisonment if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1

a) and thereby causes serious harm to health to multiple persons or death of multiple persons,

b) and thereby causes damage on a large scale or another particularly serious consequence,

c) as a member of a dangerous grouping, or

d) during a crisis situation.

Druhý diel

Criminal offenses against the security of the republic

## § 318

Espionage

(1) Whoever spies on a fact classified for the protection of interests of the Slovak Republic or for the protection of interests of another state, international organization, supranational organization or association of states, for the protection of whose interests the Slovak Republic has committed itself, marked according to law with the classification level Top Secret or Secret, with the aim of disclosing it to a foreign power or foreign agent, or whoever with such aim collects data containing such classified fact, or whoever intentionally discloses such classified fact to a foreign power, shall be punished by imprisonment for four to ten years.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for eight to fifteen years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1

a) as a member of an organization whose purpose is to spy on classified facts, or

b) although the safeguarding of the classified fact was specifically imposed on him.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for fifteen to twenty-five years or by life imprisonment if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 during a crisis situation.

Endangering a classified fact

## § 319

(1) Whoever spies on a fact classified for the protection of interests of the Slovak Republic or for the protection of interests of another state, international organization, supranational organization or association of states, for the protection of whose interests the Slovak Republic has committed itself, marked according to law with the classification level Top Secret or Secret, with the aim of disclosing it to an unauthorized person, or whoever with such aim collects data containing a classified fact, or whoever intentionally discloses such classified fact to an unauthorized person, shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years.

(2) Whoever spies on the classified fact referred to in paragraph 1 with the intent to disclose it abroad or whoever intentionally discloses such classified fact abroad, shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to eight years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2

a) although the safeguarding of the classified fact was specifically imposed on him, or

b) during a crisis situation.

## § 320

Whoever through negligence causes the disclosure of a fact classified for the protection of interests of the Slovak Republic or for the protection of interests of another state, international organization, supranational organization or association of states, for the protection of whose interests the Slovak Republic has committed itself, marked according to law with the classification level Top Secret or Secret, to an unauthorized person, or causes the loss of a document or thing containing such classified fact, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years.

ÔSMA HLAVA

CRIMINAL OFFENSES AGAINST ORDER IN PUBLIC AFFAIRS

Prvý diel

Criminal offenses against the exercise of authority of a public authority Attack on a public authority

## § 321

(1) Whoever uses violence with the intent to influence the exercise of authority of a public authority body shall be punished by imprisonment for one year to five years.

(2) The perpetrator shall be punished by imprisonment for three years to eight years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 in a more serious manner of conduct.

(3) The perpetrator shall be punished by imprisonment for seven years to twelve years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 and thereby causes serious bodily harm or death.

(4) The perpetrator shall be punished by imprisonment for twelve years to twenty years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1

a) and thereby causes serious bodily harm to several persons or death of several persons, or

b) and thereby causes damage of large extent or other particularly serious consequence.

## § 322

(1) Whoever threatens another with killing, injury to health or causing minor damage

a) with the intent to influence the exercise of authority of a public authority body, or

b) for the exercise of authority of a public authority body, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years.

(2) The perpetrator shall be punished by imprisonment for one year to five years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 in a more serious manner of conduct.

Attack on a public official

## § 323

(1) Whoever uses violence

a) with the intent to influence the exercise of authority of a public official, or

b) for the exercise of authority of a public official, shall be punished by imprisonment for one year to five years.

(2) The perpetrator shall be punished by imprisonment for three years to eight years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1

a) and thereby causes injury to health,

b) and thereby causes greater damage,

c) in a more serious manner of conduct, or

d) against a body active in criminal proceedings or a court.

(3) The perpetrator shall be punished by imprisonment for seven years to twelve years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1

a) and thereby causes serious bodily harm or death, or

b) and thereby causes considerable damage.

(4) The perpetrator shall be punished by imprisonment for twelve years to twenty-five years or by life imprisonment if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1

a) and thereby causes death of several persons, or

b) during a crisis situation.

## § 324

(1) Whoever threatens another with killing, injury to health or causing minor damage

a) with the intent to influence the exercise of authority of a public official, or

b) for the exercise of authority of a public official, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years.

(2) The perpetrator shall be punished by imprisonment for one year to five years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 in a more serious manner of conduct.

## § 325

Protection under § 321 to 324 shall also be provided to a person who acted in support or protection of a public official.

Second Part

Criminal offences of public officials

## § 326

Abuse of authority of a public official

(1) A public official who, with the intent to cause damage to another or to obtain an undue benefit for himself or another,

a) exercises his authority in a manner contrary to law,

b) exceeds his authority, or

c) fails to fulfill an obligation arising from his authority or from a court decision, shall be punished by imprisonment for two years to five years.

(2) The perpetrator shall be punished by imprisonment for four years to ten years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1

a) in a more serious manner of conduct,

b) against a protected person, or

c) from a special motive.

(3) The perpetrator shall be punished by imprisonment for seven years to twelve years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1

a) and thereby causes serious bodily harm or death,

b) and thereby causes considerable damage, or

c) in order to prevent or hinder another from exercising his fundamental rights and freedoms.

(4) The perpetrator shall be punished by imprisonment for ten years to twenty years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1

a) and thereby causes serious bodily harm to several persons or death of several persons,

b) and thereby causes damage of large extent, or

c) during a crisis situation.

## § 326a

Perversion of justice

(1) Whoever, as a judge, lay judge or arbitrator of an arbitration court, arbitrarily applies the law in adjudication and thereby harms or favours another, shall be punished by imprisonment for one year to five years.

(2) The perpetrator shall be punished by imprisonment for three years to eight years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1

a) against a protected person, or

b) from a special motive.

## § 327

Frustration of a task by a public official

(1) A public official who, in the exercise of his authority, negligently frustrates or substantially hinders the fulfillment of an important task shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years.

(2) The perpetrator shall be punished by imprisonment for one year to five years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 and thereby causes considerable damage or other particularly serious consequence.

## § 327a

A public official who negligently fails to fulfill an obligation arising from his authority in the management of state property, municipal property, property of a higher territorial unit or property of a public law institution, although he knew that he could thereby violate or endanger an interest protected by this Act, but without reasonable grounds relied that he would not cause such violation or endangerment, and

a) thereby causes damage of large extent to state property, municipal property, property of a higher territorial unit or property of a public law institution, or

b) thereby causes serious bodily harm to several persons or death of several persons, shall be punished by imprisonment for one year to five years.

Third Part

Corruption

Accepting a bribe

## § 328

(1) Whoever directly or through an intermediary accepts, demands or has promised to himself a bribe for himself or for another person, in order to act or refrain from acting in such a way as to breach his obligations arising from employment, profession, position or function, shall be punished by imprisonment for two years to five years.

(2) The perpetrator shall be punished by imprisonment for three years to eight years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 in a more serious manner of conduct.

(3) The perpetrator shall be punished by imprisonment for seven years to twelve years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 on a large scale.

## § 329

(1) Whoever, in connection with the procurement of a matter of public interest, directly or through an intermediary accepts, demands or has promised to himself a bribe for himself or for another person, shall be punished by imprisonment for three years to eight years.

(2) The perpetrator shall be punished by imprisonment for five years to twelve years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 as a public official.

(3) The perpetrator shall be punished by imprisonment for ten years to fifteen years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2 on a large scale.

## § 330

(1) Whoever, as a foreign public official, directly or through an intermediary accepts, demands or has promised to himself a bribe for himself or for another person in connection with the performance of official duties or in connection with the exercise of his function with the intent to obtain or retain an undue advantage, shall be punished by imprisonment for five years to twelve years.

(2) The perpetrator shall be punished by imprisonment for ten years to fifteen years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 on a large scale.

Bribery

## § 332

(1) Whoever directly or through an intermediary promises, offers or provides a bribe to another in order that he act or refrain from acting in such a way as to breach his obligations arising from employment, profession, position or function, or for this reason directly or through an intermediary promises, offers or provides a bribe to another person, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years.

(2) The perpetrator shall be punished by imprisonment for one year to five years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 in a more serious manner of conduct.

(3) The perpetrator shall be punished by imprisonment for four years to ten years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 on a large scale.

## § 333
