(1) Whoever provides, offers or promises a bribe to another in connection with the procurement of a matter of public interest directly or through an intermediary, or for this reason provides, offers or promises a bribe to another person, shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years.

(2) The perpetrator shall be punished by imprisonment for two years to five years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1

a) in a more serious manner of conduct, or

b) towards a public official.

(3) The perpetrator shall be punished by imprisonment for five years to twelve years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 on a large scale.

## § 334

(1) Whoever directly or through an intermediary provides, offers or promises a bribe to a foreign public official or other person in connection with the performance of official duties or in connection with the performance of his function as a foreign public official with the intent to obtain or maintain an undue advantage, shall be punished by imprisonment for two to five years.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for five to twelve years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 on a large scale.

## § 336

Indirect corruption

(1) Whoever directly or through an intermediary for himself or for another person accepts, requests or allows to be promised a bribe for the fact that the bribed person will use his influence or through the influence of another act, or for the fact that he acted

a) on the exercise of authority of a public official,

b) on the exercise of authority of a foreign public official,

c) on a person in connection with the procurement of a matter of general interest or

d) on a person to act or refrain from acting in such a way as to violate his obligations arising from employment, profession, position or function, shall be punished by imprisonment for three to eight years.

(2) Whoever directly or through an intermediary promises, offers or provides to another a bribe for the fact that the bribed person will use his influence or through the influence of another act, or for the fact that he acted

a) on the exercise of authority of a public official,

b) on the exercise of authority of a foreign public official,

c) on a person in connection with the procurement of a matter of general interest or

d) on a person to act or refrain from acting in such a way as to violate his obligations arising from employment, profession, position or function, shall be punished by imprisonment for two to five years.

## § 336a

Electoral corruption

(1) Whoever directly or through an intermediary provides, offers or promises a bribe to someone who has the right to vote, participate in a referendum or in a popular vote on the recall of the President of the Slovak Republic, in order to

a) vote or cast a vote in a certain way,

b) not vote or not cast a vote in a certain way,

c) not vote or not cast a vote at all, or

d) not participate in elections, a referendum or a popular vote on the recall of the President of the Slovak Republic, or for this reason directly or through an intermediary provides, offers or promises a bribe to another person, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years.

(2) Whoever in connection with the exercise of the right to vote, participate in a referendum or in a popular vote on the recall of the President of the Slovak Republic directly or through an intermediary for himself or another person accepts, requests or allows to be promised a bribe, in order to

a) vote or cast a vote in a certain way,

b) not vote or not cast a vote in a certain way,

c) not vote or not cast a vote at all, or

| | d) not participate in elections, a referendum or a popular vote on the recall of the President of the Slovak Republic,shall be punished by imprisonment for up to one year.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1

a) in a more serious manner of conduct,

b) as a public official,

c) on a protected person, or

d) publicly.

## § 336b

Sports corruption

(1) Whoever directly or through an intermediary promises, offers or provides a bribe to another in order to act or refrain from acting so as to influence the course of a competition or the result of a competition, shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years.

(2) In the same manner as in paragraph 1 shall be punished whoever directly or through an intermediary for himself or for another person accepts, requests or allows to be promised a bribe in order to act or refrain from acting and thus influence the course or result of a competition.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two to eight years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 or paragraph 2

a) although he was convicted for such an act in the preceding twenty-four months or was penalized for a similar act in the preceding twenty-four months,

b) in a more serious manner of conduct or

c) on a larger scale.

(4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for four to ten years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 or paragraph 2

a) as a referee, delegate of a sports association or official of a sports organization,

b) in a competition organized by an international sports organization or

c) on a considerable scale.

(5) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for five to twelve years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 or paragraph 2 on a large scale.

Acceptance and provision of undue advantage

## § 336c

(1) Whoever as a public official directly or through an intermediary for himself or for another person accepts, requests or allows to be promised an undue advantage in connection with his position or function, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 in relation to an undue advantage of greater value.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to eight years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 in relation to an undue advantage of the value of a large scale.

## § 336d

(1) Whoever directly or through an intermediary provides, offers or promises to a public official or other person an undue advantage in connection with the position or function of a public official, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one to three years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 in relation to an undue advantage of greater value.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two to five years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 in relation to an undue advantage of the value of a large scale.

Part Four

Some forms of criminal complicity

## § 337

Incitement

Whoever publicly incites to a criminal offense or publicly calls for mass non-fulfillment of an important obligation imposed by law or on its basis, or for serious breach of public order, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years.

## § 338

Approval of a criminal offense

(1) Whoever publicly approves of a criminal offense or publicly praises the offender for a criminal offense, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to one year.

(2) In the same manner as in paragraph 1 shall be punished whoever with the intent to express agreement with a criminal offense

a) rewards or compensates the offender or a person close to him for the punishment, or

b) collects funds for such reward or compensation.

## § 339

Harboring

(1) Whoever helps the offender of a criminal offense with the intent to enable him to escape criminal prosecution, punishment or protective measure or their execution, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years; however, if he thus helps the offender of a criminal offense for which this law establishes a more lenient punishment, he shall be punished by this more lenient punishment.

(2) Whoever commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 in favor of a person close to him is not punishable except in the case that he would do so with the intent to

a) help a person who committed the criminal offense of treason under § 311, subversion against the Slovak Republic under § 312, terror under § 313 and § 314, sabotage under § 315 and § 316, sabotage under § 317, espionage under § 318 or genocide under § 418, or

b) procure for himself or another pecuniary gain.

(3) Whoever commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 is not punishable if he was forced to provide help and could not refuse it without exposing himself or a close person to danger of death, injury to health or other serious harm.

## § 340

Failure to Report a Criminal Offense

(1) Whoever learns in a credible manner that another person has committed a felony for which this Act establishes a custodial sentence with an upper limit of at least ten years, or any of the corruption offenses specified in Part Three of Chapter Eight of the Special Part, and fails to report such felony or offense without delay to an authority active in criminal proceedings or to the Police Force, or instead of that, in the case of a soldier, to his superior or service authority, and in the case of a person serving a custodial sentence or in detention to a member of the Prison and Court Guard Service, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years.

(2) Whoever commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 shall not be criminally liable if he could not make the report without exposing himself or a close person to the danger of death, bodily harm, other serious detriment or criminal prosecution.

(3) Whoever commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 shall not be criminally liable if by reporting the criminal offense he would violate

a) the confessional secrecy or the secrecy of information that was entrusted to him orally or in writing under the condition of confidentiality as a person entrusted with pastoral activity, or

b) a duty of confidentiality recognized by law.

## § 341

Failure to Prevent a Criminal Offense

(1) Whoever learns in a credible manner that another person is preparing or committing a felony for which this Act establishes a custodial sentence with an upper limit of the sentencing range of at least ten years, or any of the corruption offenses specified in Part Three of Chapter Eight of the Special Part, and fails to prevent the commission or completion of such felony or offense personally or through another capable person, or the competent authority, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years.

(2) Whoever commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 shall not be criminally liable if he could not prevent the felony without considerable difficulty or without exposing himself or a close person to the danger of death, serious bodily harm, other serious detriment or criminal prosecution. Exposing a close person to the danger of criminal prosecution, however, does not exempt the offender from criminal liability if it relates to the failure to prevent a felony for which this Act allows the imposition of a life sentence.

(3) A felony may be prevented also by its timely report to an authority active in criminal proceedings or to the Police Force; a soldier may instead make the report to a superior or service authority and a person serving a custodial sentence or in detention to a member of the Prison and Court Guard Service.

(4) The obligation pursuant to paragraph 1 does not apply to a person if by fulfilling it she would violate the confessional secrecy.

Fifth Part

Other Forms of Disrupting the Activities of Public Authorities

## § 342

Interference with the Independence of a Court

(1) Whoever influences a judge not to fulfill his duty in proceedings before a court, or whoever acts with the intent to thwart the rights of participants in judicial proceedings or of the accused to a lawful judge, shall be punished by imprisonment for one to six years.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two to eight years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1

a) from a special motive,

b) in order to thwart or hinder another person's exercise of his fundamental rights and freedoms,

c) as a public official or other person in connection with the performance of his employment, profession, position or function, or

d) as a person exercising the authority of a court management and administration body.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to ten years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 with the intent to obtain for himself or another person a substantial benefit or to cause substantial damage or other particularly serious consequence.

## § 343

Contempt of Court

Whoever by repeated conduct despite prior warning

a) disrupts court proceedings in a serious manner,

b) behaves insultingly towards the court at a hearing or disparages the court, or

c) without sufficient excuse disobeys an order of the court or its summons, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years.

## § 344

Obstruction of Justice

(1) Whoever in proceedings before a court or in criminal proceedings

a) presents evidence which he knows to be forged or altered, for the purpose of using it as genuine,

b) forges, alters or destroys evidence, or prevents the obtaining of evidence,

c) thwarts or prevents the presence or testimony of a party to criminal proceedings, a participant in judicial proceedings, or their representatives in the proceedings, a witness, expert, interpreter or translator, or

d) uses violence, threat of violence or threat of other serious detriment to influence a judge, a party to criminal proceedings, a participant in judicial proceedings, a witness, expert, interpreter, translator or an authority active in criminal proceedings, shall be punished by imprisonment for one to six years.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to eight years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1

a) with the intent to obtain for himself or another person a substantial benefit or to cause substantial damage or other particularly serious consequence,

b) with the intent to thwart or hinder another person's exercise of his fundamental rights and freedoms,

c) from a special motive,

d) as a public official or other person in connection with the performance of his employment, profession, position or function, or

e) as a person exercising the authority of a court management and administration body.

## § 345

False Accusation

(1) Whoever falsely accuses another person of a criminal offense with the intent to cause his criminal prosecution shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to eight years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1

a) from a special motive, or

b) publicly.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for four to ten years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 and thereby causes substantial damage or other particularly serious consequence.

## § 346

False Testimony and Perjury

(1) Whoever as a witness in proceedings before a court or in criminal proceedings or for the purposes of criminal proceedings abroad before a prosecutor or a police officer, or before a judge of an international body recognized by the Slovak Republic, states an untruth about a circumstance which is of substantial importance for the decision, or whoever conceals such a circumstance, shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years.

(2) Whoever in criminal proceedings before a court or for the purposes of criminal proceedings abroad after taking an oath states an untruth about a circumstance which is of substantial importance for the decision, or conceals such a circumstance, shall be punished by imprisonment for two to five years.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to eight years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2

a) by a more serious manner of conduct, or

b) from a special motive.

(4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for four to ten years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2 and thereby causes substantial damage or other particularly serious consequence.

## § 347

False Expert Opinion, Interpreting Act and Translation Act

(1) Whoever as an expert, interpreter or translator

a) before a court, prosecutor or police officer in criminal proceedings or before a court in civil proceedings and in administrative judicial proceedings, or in enforcement proceedings, or in proceedings before a public administration body, or before an arbitration tribunal, states an untruth about a circumstance which is of substantial importance for the decision, or conceals such a circumstance, or

b) when providing an expert opinion or performing an interpreting or translation act on the basis of a contract causes minor damage to another person by stating an untruth about a circumstance which is of substantial importance for the person to whom the expert opinion or interpreting or translation act relates, or is of substantial importance for the decision of which the expert opinion or interpreting or translation act is the basis, or if he conceals such a circumstance, shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to eight years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1

a) by a more serious manner of conduct, or

b) from a special motive.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for four to ten years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 and thereby causes substantial damage or other particularly serious consequence.

Obstruction of Execution of Official Decision

## § 348

(1) Who obstructs or substantially hampers the execution of a decision of a court or other public authority by:

a) without serious reason failing to commence the execution of a sentence of imprisonment within the time limit set by the court,

b) without permission and without serious reason staying in a place or district to which the sentence of prohibition of residence applies, or failing to comply with restrictions and obligations imposed by the court in connection with the execution of this sentence,

c) without permission and without serious reason unlawfully staying in the territory of the Slovak Republic, although a sentence of expulsion was imposed on him or he was prohibited from staying in the territory of the Slovak Republic,

d) performing an activity to which a decision of a court or other state authority on prohibition of activity applies,

e) despite a final decision of a court prohibiting the receipt of grants, subsidies or assistance and support provided from European Union funds, applying for or receiving a grant, subsidy or assistance and support provided from European Union funds, or despite a final decision of a court prohibiting participation in public procurement, participating in public procurement,

f) committing serious conduct to frustrate the purpose of protective treatment or protective education imposed on him by the court, or otherwise, especially by escape from an institution, substantially hampering the execution of such decisions,

g) committing serious conduct to frustrate the purpose of custody or sentence,

h) committing serious or repeated conduct to frustrate eviction from common dwelling issued under a special regulation or on the basis of an urgent measure of the court,

i) committing serious or repeated conduct to frustrate a prohibition or restriction of contact, entry or approach issued and certified under a special regulation or issued on the basis of a court decision in civil proceedings,

j) participating in a public event to which the sentence of prohibition of participation in public events applies or failing to comply with restrictions and obligations imposed by the court in connection with the execution of this sentence,

k) committing serious conduct by which he frustrates the execution of imposed protective supervision, or

l) despite a final decision prohibiting continuation of works, modifications or implementation of construction or its change, performing works, modifications or otherwise continuing in the implementation of construction or its change, where it is not a simple construction or minor construction under construction regulations,

shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years.

(2) Who frustrates or substantially hampers in criminal proceedings the execution of a decision of a court or other public authority by:

a) destroying, damaging, rendering unusable, concealing, stealing or removing a thing to which such decision relates, or

b) escaping from guard, from custody or from execution of imprisonment or assisting another in such escape,

shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years.

## § 349

Who, after measures in civil proceedings aimed at executing a court decision or a court-approved agreement on the upbringing of minor children have been used against him without result, frustrates the execution of such decision or agreement, or who frustrates the execution of an urgent measure imposed in civil proceedings for the protection of persons threatened by violence or in matters of court care for minors, shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years.

## § 350

Obstruction of Execution of a Decision of the Constitutional Court of the Slovak Republic

Who obstructs or substantially hampers the execution of a decision of the Constitutional Court of the Slovak Republic by failing to fulfill an obligation arising from its decision, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years.

## § 351

Obstruction of Preparation and Course of Elections and Referendum

(1) Who by violence or threat of violence, or by fraud prevents another from exercising the constitutional right to vote or to vote in a referendum, or forces another in such a way to exercise such constitutional rights, shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years.

(2) In the same manner as in paragraph 1 shall be punished who, in connection with the exercise of constitutional rights referred to in paragraph 1:

a) knowingly incorrectly counts votes or violates the secrecy of voting, or

b) otherwise in a gross manner frustrates the exercise of these constitutional rights.

(3) By imprisonment for one to five years the offender shall be punished if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2:

a) in a more serious manner of conduct,

b) as a public official,

c) from a special motive, or

d) publicly.

## § 352

Falsification and Alteration of Public Document, Official Seal, Official Closure, Official Emblem and Official Mark

(1) Who falsifies a public document, official seal, official closure, official emblem, official mark or stamp, substantially alters their content with the intention that they be used as genuine, or uses them as genuine, or has them made with the intention to use them as genuine, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years.

(2) In the same manner as in paragraph 1 shall be punished who obtains the issuance of a genuine public document, official seal, official closure, official emblem, official mark or stamp on the basis of false documents as well.

(3) By imprisonment for one to five years the offender shall be punished if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2 in a more serious manner.

(4) By imprisonment for three to eight years the offender shall be punished if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2:

a) and thereby causes considerable damage, or

b) from a special motive.

(5) By imprisonment for four to ten years the offender shall be punished if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2 and thereby causes damage of large extent or other particularly serious consequence.

(6) By imprisonment for seven to twelve years the offender shall be punished if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 or 2 as a member of a dangerous grouping.

(7) Protection under paragraphs 1 to 6 shall also be provided to foreign public documents, official seals, official closures, official emblems or official marks.

## § 352a

Falsification and Preparation of False Medical Documentation

(1) Who falsifies medical documentation or intentionally prepares false medical documentation with the intention to use it as genuine in proceedings before a public authority, or uses it as genuine in proceedings before a public authority, or who has such medical documentation prepared with the intention to use it as genuine in proceedings before a public authority, or uses such medical documentation as genuine in proceedings before a public authority, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years.

(2) By imprisonment for six months to three years the offender shall be punished if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1:

a) and thereby obtains for himself or for another greater benefit,

b) and thereby causes greater damage,

c) as a health care worker,

d) in a more serious manner of conduct, or

e) from a special motive.

(3) By imprisonment for one to five years the offender shall be punished if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 and:

a) thereby obtains for himself or for another considerable benefit, or

b) thereby causes considerable damage.

(4) By imprisonment for three to eight years the offender shall be punished if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 and:

a) thereby obtains for himself or for another large benefit, or

b) thereby causes damage of large extent.

## § 353

Endangering Confidential Fact and Restricted Fact

(1) Who spies on a fact classified for the protection of interests of the Slovak Republic or for the protection of interests of another state, international organization, supranational organization or association of states, for the protection of whose interests the Slovak Republic has committed itself, marked according to law with the classification level Confidential or Restricted, with the aim to disclose it to an unauthorized person, or who with such aim collects data containing such classified fact, or who intentionally discloses such classified fact to an unauthorized person, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to one year.

(2) Who spies on a classified fact referred to in paragraph 1 with the intention to disclose it abroad or who intentionally discloses such classified fact abroad, shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years.

## § 354

Violent Crossing of State Border

(1) Who crosses the state border using violence or threat of imminent violence shall be punished by imprisonment for three to eight years.

(2) By imprisonment for seven to twelve years the offender shall be punished if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1

a) by a more serious manner of conduct,

b) with at least two persons, or

c) by organizing such act.

(3) By imprisonment for twelve to twenty years the offender shall be punished if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1

a) and causes thereby serious bodily harm or death, or

b) during a crisis situation.

(4) By imprisonment for twenty to twenty-five years or by life imprisonment the offender shall be punished if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1

a) and causes thereby serious bodily harm to multiple persons or death of multiple persons, or

b) as a soldier during a crisis situation.

Smuggling of Migrants

## § 355

(1) Who for a person who is not a citizen of the Slovak Republic or a person with permanent residence in the territory of the Slovak Republic, organizes unauthorized crossing of the state border of the Slovak Republic or passage through its territory, or enables such conduct, or assists in it, shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years.

(2) Who with the intent to obtain for himself or another directly or indirectly a financial advantage or other material advantage for a person who is not a citizen of the Slovak Republic or a person with permanent residence in the territory of the Slovak Republic,

a) organizes unauthorized crossing of the state border of the Slovak Republic or passage through its territory, or enables such conduct, or assists in it, or

b) for the purpose according to letter a) manufactures, procures, provides or holds a false travel document or false identity document, shall be punished by imprisonment for three to eight years.

(3) By imprisonment for seven to ten years the offender shall be punished if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 or 2

a) and obtains thereby for himself or another greater benefit,

b) from a special motive,

c) in a manner that may endanger the lives and health of smuggled persons or means inhuman treatment or degrading treatment, or abuse of smuggled persons, or

d) by a more serious manner of conduct.

(4) By imprisonment for ten to fifteen years the offender shall be punished if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 or 2

a) and causes thereby serious bodily harm or death,

b) and obtains thereby for himself or another substantial benefit, or

c) as a member of a dangerous grouping.

(5) By imprisonment for twelve to twenty years the offender shall be punished if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 or 2

a) and causes thereby serious bodily harm to multiple persons or death of multiple persons,

b) and obtains thereby benefit on a large scale, or

c) during a crisis situation.

## § 356

Who with the intent to obtain for himself or another directly or indirectly a financial advantage or other material advantage for a person who is not a citizen of the Slovak Republic or a citizen of another Member State of the European Union or a citizen of a contracting state of the Agreement on the European Economic Area, or does not have permanent residence in their territory, enables or assists to remain or to be unlawfully employed in the territory of the Slovak Republic, another Member State of the European Union or a contracting state of the Agreement on the European Economic Area, shall be punished by imprisonment for two to eight years.

## § 357

Unauthorized Crossing of State Border

Who violates regulations on international flights by entering the territory of the Slovak Republic by aircraft shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years.

## § 358

Mutiny of Prisoners

(1) Who participates in resistance of a group of prisoners against a supervisory authority, its order or prison regulations shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years.

(2) By imprisonment for four to ten years the offender shall be punished if he organizes the act specified in paragraph 1.

NINTH PART

CRIMINAL OFFENCES AGAINST OTHER RIGHTS AND FREEDOMS

## § 359

Violence Against a Group of Inhabitants

(1) Who threatens a group of inhabitants with death, serious bodily harm or other serious harm, or causing damage on a large scale, or who uses violence against a group of inhabitants, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years.

(2) By imprisonment for six months to three years the offender shall be punished if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1

a) from a special motive,

b) by a more serious manner of conduct, or

c) publicly.

## § 360

Dangerous Threatening

(1) Who threatens another with death, serious bodily harm or other serious harm in such a manner that it may arouse reasonable fear shall be punished by imprisonment for up to one year.

(2) By imprisonment for six months to three years the offender shall be punished if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1

a) by a more serious manner of conduct,

b) on a protected person,

c) in order to thwart or hinder another in the exercise of his fundamental rights and freedoms,

d) from a special motive, or

e) publicly.

## § 360a

Dangerous Stalking

(1) Who stalks another for a long time in such a manner that it may arouse reasonable fear for his life or health, life or health of a person close to him or substantially worsen the quality of his life, by

a) threatening bodily harm or other harm to him or a person close to him,

b) seeking his personal proximity or following him,

c) contacting him through a third person or electronic communication service, in writing or otherwise against his will,

d) misusing his personal data for the purpose of obtaining personal or other contact, or

e) otherwise restricting him in his usual way of life, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to one year.

(2) By imprisonment for six months to three years the offender shall be punished if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1

a) on a protected person,

b) by a more serious manner of conduct, | | c) from a special motive, or

d) publicly.

## § 360b

Dangerous Electronic Harassment

(1) Who intentionally through an electronic communication service, computer system or computer network substantially worsens the quality of life of another by

a) humiliating him for a long time, intimidating him, acting in his name without authorization or otherwise harassing him for a long time, or

b) unlawfully publishing or making available to a third person a visual, audio or audiovisual recording of his expression of a personal nature obtained with his consent, capable of substantially endangering his dignity or causing him other serious harm to his rights, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years.

(2) By imprisonment for one to four years the offender shall be punished if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1

a) on a protected person, or

b) from a special motive.

(3) By imprisonment for two to six years the offender shall be punished if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1

a) and causes thereby substantial damage,

b) with the intent to obtain for himself or another substantial benefit, or

c) although he was convicted for such act in the preceding twenty-four months.

Spreading of Alarming Message

## § 361

(1) Who intentionally causes a danger of serious disturbance of at least part of the population of a place by spreading an alarming message that is false, or commits other similar conduct capable of causing such danger, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years.

(2) Who reports a message or other similar conduct specified in paragraph 1, although he knows that they are false and may trigger a measure leading to a danger of serious disturbance of at least part of the population of a place, to a legal person or the Police Force or another state authority or mass media, shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years.

(3) By imprisonment for three to eight years the offender shall be punished if he commits the act specified in paragraph 2

a) and has already been convicted for such act, or

b) and causes thereby serious disruption in the economic operation or economic activity of a legal person or in the activity of a state authority or other particularly serious consequence.

## § 362

Whoever, during a state crisis situation, causes, even through negligence, a danger of serious disturbance, faint-heartedness or defeatist mood in at least part of the population of any place by spreading alarming news, shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years.

## § 363

Intoxication

(1) Whoever, by consumption or application of an addictive substance, even through negligence, brings himself into a state of insanity in which he commits conduct which otherwise has the characteristics of a criminal offence, shall be punished by imprisonment for three years to eight years; however, if he commits conduct which otherwise has the characteristics of a criminal offence for which the law provides a milder punishment, he shall be punished with this milder punishment.

(2) The provision of paragraph 1, nor § 23, shall not apply if the offender brought himself into a state of insanity with the intention of committing a criminal offence.

## § 364

Hooliganism

(1) Whoever commits verbally or physically, publicly or in a place accessible to the public, gross indecency or hooliganism, in particular by

a) assaulting another,

b) defaming a state symbol,

c) defaming a historical or cultural monument,

d) grossly disrupting the course of a public event, in particular by unauthorized use of a pyrotechnic product, violent damage to a seat or part of a sports facility serving to separate sectors, throwing a dangerous object onto a sports field or onto another place of the event, or

e) causing public scandal by performing sexual intercourse or by performing sexual exhibitionism or other pathological sexual practices, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1

a) from a special motive,

b) by a more serious manner of conduct,

c) in the presence of a group of persons younger than eighteen years,

d) on a protected person, or

e) although he was convicted for such or similar act in the preceding twenty-four months or sanctioned in the preceding twelve months.

## § 365

Desecration of place of final rest

(1) Whoever destroys, damages or desecrates a grave, an urn with human remains, a monument or a tombstone, or destroys or damages the equipment of a burial ground or other place of final rest, or whoever commits other gross indecency or hooliganism at a burial ground or other place of final rest, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1

a) by a more serious manner of conduct, or

b) from a special motive.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one year to five years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 and causes considerable damage thereby.

## § 366

Desecration of the dead

(1) Whoever abuses or desecrates the dead, or unauthorizedly carries out exhumation of human remains, or arbitrarily removes human remains from a burial ground, or handles human remains in violation of a generally binding legal regulation, shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one year to five years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1

a) by a more serious manner of conduct, or

b) from a special motive.

## § 367

Procuring

(1) Whoever procures, incites, seduces, exploits, recruits or offers another for the performance of prostitution, or whoever profits from prostitution performed by another, or enables its performance, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one year to five years if he commits such act by a more serious manner of conduct.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three years to ten years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 on a protected person.

(4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for seven years to twelve years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1

a) and obtains considerable benefit for himself or for another thereby, or

b) as a member of a dangerous grouping.

(5) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for ten years to fifteen years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 and causes serious harm to health or death thereby.

## § 368

Production of child pornography

(1) Whoever exploits, recruits, offers or otherwise abuses a child for the production of child pornography or child pornographic performance or enables such abuse, or otherwise participates in such production, shall be punished by imprisonment for four years to ten years.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for seven years to twelve years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1

a) on a child younger than twelve years,

b) by a more serious manner of conduct,

c) publicly,

d) by joint conduct of at least two persons, or

e) and directly endangers the life of the child thereby.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for ten years to fifteen years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1

a) and causes serious harm to health or death thereby, or

b) and obtains considerable benefit thereby.

(4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for twelve years to twenty years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1

a) and causes serious harm to health to several persons or death of several persons thereby,

b) and obtains benefit of large scale thereby, or

c) as a member of a dangerous grouping.

## § 369

Distribution of child pornography

(1) Whoever reproduces, transports, procures, makes accessible or otherwise distributes child pornography, shall be punished by imprisonment for one year to five years.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three years to eight years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1

a) by a more serious manner of conduct, or

b) publicly.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for four years to ten years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 and obtains considerable benefit thereby.

(4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for seven years to twelve years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 and obtains benefit of large scale thereby.

## § 370

Possession of child pornography and participation in child pornographic performance

(1) Whoever possesses child pornography or whoever acts with the intention to obtain access to child pornography through an electronic communication service, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years.

(2) In the same way as in paragraph 1 shall be punished whoever intentionally participates in a child pornographic performance.

Endangering morality

## § 371

(1) Whoever produces, purchases, imports or otherwise procures and subsequently sells, lends or otherwise puts into circulation, distributes, makes publicly accessible or publishes pornography, sound or image carriers, depictions or other objects endangering morality, in which disrespect for the human being and violence is manifested or which depict sexual intercourse with an animal or other sexual pathological practices, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one year to five years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1

a) by a more serious manner of conduct, or

b) publicly.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three years to eight years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1 and obtains considerable benefit thereby.

## § 372

(1) Whoever pornography

a) offers, surrenders or sells to a child, or

b) exhibits or otherwise makes accessible in a place accessible to children, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one year to five years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1

a) by a more serious manner of conduct, or

b) publicly.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three years to eight years if he commits the act referred to in paragraph 1

a) and obtains considerable benefit for himself or for another, or

b) by offering, making accessible or exhibiting pornography, sound or image carriers or depictions, in which disrespect for the human being and violence is manifested or which depict sexual intercourse with an animal or other sexual pathological practices.

## § 372a

Support and Promotion of Sexual Pathological Practices

(1) Whoever supports or publicly or in a place accessible to the public promotes, approves, grossly trivializes or attempts to justify sexual intercourse

a) with a child,

b) with a relative in direct line or with a sibling,

c) with a dead person,

d) with an animal, or

e) other sexual pathological practices, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years.

(2) The same as in subsection 1 shall be punished whoever supports or publicly or in a place accessible to the public promotes, approves, grossly trivializes or attempts to justify pedophilia, necrophilia or zoophilia.

(3) Whoever commits the act specified in subsection 2 is not criminally liable if the promotion was demonstrably carried out for the purpose of educational, therapeutic or research activities.

(4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years if he commits the act specified in subsection 1 or 2

a) and thereby obtains substantial benefit,

b) and thereby causes serious harm to the rights of the affected person,

c) although he was convicted for such or similar act in the preceding twenty-four months or sanctioned in the preceding twelve months, or

d) by a more serious manner of conduct.

## § 373

Defamation

(1) Whoever communicates about another a false statement which is capable of substantially endangering his reputation among fellow citizens, harming him in employment, in business, disrupting his family relations or causing him other serious harm, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years if he commits the act specified in subsection 1

a) and thereby causes substantial damage,

b) from a special motive,

c) publicly, or

d) in business by a more serious manner of conduct.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to eight years if he commits the act specified in subsection 1

a) and thereby causes damage of large extent, or

b) and causes another loss of employment, bankruptcy of a business or divorce.

## § 374

Unauthorized Handling of Personal Data

(1) Whoever unlawfully provides, makes accessible or discloses

a) personal data about another collected in connection with the exercise of public authority or the exercise of constitutional rights of the person, or

b) personal data about another obtained in connection with the exercise of his profession, employment or function and thereby violates a duty established by a generally binding legal regulation, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to one year.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years if he commits the act specified in subsection 1

a) and thereby causes serious harm to the rights of the affected person,

b) publicly, or

c) by a more serious manner of conduct.

Damaging the Rights of Others

## § 375

(1) Whoever causes another serious harm to rights by

a) misleading someone or

b) exploiting someone's error, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years if he commits the act specified in subsection 1

a) by a more serious manner of conduct,

b) against a protected person, or

c) by impersonating a public official.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years if he commits the act specified in subsection 1 and thereby obtains for himself or for another substantial benefit.

## § 376

Whoever unlawfully violates the secrecy of a letter or other written document, sound recording, image recording or other recording, computer data or other document kept in the privacy of another by disclosing or making them accessible to a third party or otherwise using them and thereby causes another serious harm to rights, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years.

## § 377

Violation of Confidentiality of Oral Expression and Other Expression of Personal Nature

(1) Whoever violates the confidentiality of privately spoken words or other expression of personal nature by unlawfully capturing it with a recording device and making the recording thus made accessible to a third party or otherwise using it and thereby causes another serious harm to rights, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years if he commits the act specified in subsection 1

a) as a member of an organized group,

b) and causes by such act substantial damage, or

c) with intent to obtain for himself or another substantial benefit.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to five years if

a) he commits the act specified in subsection 1 as a public official,

b) causes by such act damage of large extent, or

c) commits such act with intent to obtain for himself or for another benefit of large extent.

TENTH CHAPTER

CRIMINAL OFFENCES AGAINST MILITARY DUTY, AGAINST CIVIL SERVICE, AGAINST SERVICE IN THE ARMED FORCES AND AGAINST DEFENCE OF THE HOMELAND First Part

Criminal Offences Against Military Duty

## § 379

Frustrating Fitness for Service

(1) Whoever renders himself or another permanently or temporarily unfit or less fit for the performance of military duty or other duties for the defence of the homeland, shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to five years.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to ten years if he commits the act specified in subsection 1 during a crisis situation.

## § 380

Failure to Fulfill Conscription Duty

(1) Whoever fails to report for conscription or fails to undergo medical examination with intent not to fulfill his conscription duty, or to delay its fulfillment, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to one year.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years if he commits the act specified in subsection 1 during a crisis situation.

## § 381

Evading Military Duty

(1) Whoever commits machinations with intent to obtain relief in the performance of military duty, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to one year.

(2) Whoever commits machinations with intent to

a) himself wholly or partly evade the performance of military duty, or

b) enable another to wholly or partly evade the performance of military duty, shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years if he commits the act specified in subsection 1 or 2 during a crisis situation. Second Part

Criminal Offences Against Civil Service

Failure to Commence Civil Service

## § 382

Whoever with intent to evade civil service fails to commence it within 24 hours after the expiry of the period specified in the conscription order, shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years.

## § 383

Whoever fails to commence, even through negligence, civil service within 24 hours after the expiry of the period specified in the conscription order, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to one year.

Evading the Performance of Civil Service

## § 384

(1) Whoever with intent to evade the performance of civil service or the fulfillment of duties arising therefrom injures his health, feigns illness, falsifies a document, abuses an addictive substance or uses another stratagem, or whoever refuses to perform civil service, shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years.

(2) Whoever refuses to fulfill instructions in the performance of civil service or systematically fails to fulfill such instructions, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years.

## § 385

(1) Whoever without permission leaves the place of performance of civil service for a period longer than 48 hours, or stays without permission outside this place for such period, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to six months.

(2) Whoever without permission leaves the place of performance of civil service for a period longer than 14 days, or stays without permission outside this place for such period, shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years.

(3) Whoever with intent to evade the performance of civil service leaves the place of performance of this service or with this intent stays outside this place, shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to five years.

Third Part

Criminal Offences Against Service in the Armed Forces Failure to Commence Service in the Armed Forces

## § 386

(1) Whoever with intent to evade military service fails to commence service in the armed forces within 24 hours after the expiry of the period specified in the conscription order, shall be punished by imprisonment for two to five years.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for five to ten years if he commits the act specified in subsection 1 during a crisis situation.

## § 387

(1) Whoever fails to report for service in the armed forces, even through negligence, within 24 hours after the expiry of the deadline specified in the call-up order, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 during a crisis situation.

## § 388

Whoever, after the declaration of mobilization of the armed forces, even through negligence, fails to report from abroad without delay to commence military service, shall be punished by imprisonment for one year to five years.

## § 389

Violation of personal and material obligations

(1) Whoever, after the declaration of mobilization of the armed forces, fails to fulfill, even through negligence, a personal or material obligation, or the obligation to provide accommodation for the defense of the homeland, or intentionally evades the fulfillment of such obligation, or frustrates or hinders, even through negligence, the fulfillment of such obligation by another person, shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two years to eight years if he endangers the interests of the defense of the homeland to a significant extent by the act specified in paragraph 1.

Štvrtý diel

Criminal Offenses Against the Defense of the Homeland

## § 390

Cooperation with the enemy

Whoever, during a state of war or war, procures benefit for the enemy or supports him in any way, shall be punished, unless it is an act subject to more severe punishment, by imprisonment for one year to ten years.

## § 391

War treason

A citizen of the Slovak Republic who, during a state of war or war, serves in an enemy army or in an enemy armed corps, shall be punished by imprisonment for ten years to twenty-five years or by life imprisonment.

## § 392

Service in a foreign army

(1) A citizen of the Slovak Republic who serves in a foreign army without permission shall be punished by imprisonment for two years to eight years.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for five years to ten years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 during a state of war or war.

JEDENÁSTA HLAVA

MILITARY CRIMINAL OFFENSES

Prvý diel

Criminal Offenses Against Military Subordination and Military Honor

Disobedience to order

## § 393

(1) Whoever refuses to execute or intentionally fails to execute an order shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two years to eight years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1

a) with a group of soldiers,

b) with a weapon,

c) under circumstances where he could thereby frustrate or substantially hinder the fulfillment of an important service task, or

d) as a member of the armed forces deployed outside the territory of the Slovak Republic.

(3) The offender shall be punished in the same manner as in paragraph 2 if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1

a) and thereby causes serious bodily harm or death, or

b) and thereby causes endangerment of the combat readiness of a unit or another particularly serious consequence.

(4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for five years to fifteen years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 during a crisis situation.

## § 394

(1) Whoever through negligence fails to execute an order under circumstances where he could thereby frustrate or substantially hinder the fulfillment of an important service task shall be punished by imprisonment for up to one year.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to five years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1

a) and thereby causes serious bodily harm or death,

b) and thereby causes endangerment of the combat readiness of a unit or another particularly serious consequence, or

c) as a member of the armed forces deployed outside the territory of the Slovak Republic.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three years to ten years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 during a crisis situation.

## § 395

Resistance or coercion to violate military duty

(1) Whoever resists a soldier who is fulfilling specifically assigned military duties, or coerces him to violate such duty, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two years to seven years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1

a) and thereby causes serious bodily harm or another particularly serious consequence,

b) with a weapon,

c) with a group of soldiers, or

d) as a member of the armed forces deployed outside the territory of the Slovak Republic.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for five years to fifteen years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1

a) and thereby causes death, or

b) during a crisis situation.

Insult between soldiers

## § 396

(1) Whoever grossly insults another soldier, unless it is a more serious criminal offense, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to one year.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to five years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 during a crisis situation.

## § 397

(1) Whoever insults by violence or threat of imminent violence a soldier

a) who is a superior or of higher rank, or

b) who is a subordinate or of lower rank, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to five years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1

a) and thereby causes bodily harm,

b) at a time when he himself or the insulted soldier is performing service,

c) against a military guard or military police,

d) as a member of the armed forces deployed outside the territory of the Slovak Republic, or

e) with a weapon or with at least two persons.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three years to ten years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1

a) and thereby causes death, or

b) during a crisis situation.

## § 398

(1) Whoever insults by violence or threat of imminent violence a soldier of equal rank shall be punished by imprisonment for up to one year.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to five years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1

a) and thereby causes bodily harm,

b) at a time when he himself or the insulted soldier is performing service,

c) against a military guard or military police,

d) as a member of the armed forces deployed outside the territory of the Slovak Republic, or

e) with a weapon, or with at least two persons.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three years to ten years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1

a) and thereby causes death, or

b) during a crisis situation.

## § 399

Violence against a superior

(1) Whoever uses violence, threat of imminent violence or threat of other serious harm against a superior,

a) with the intention of influencing the performance of his military duties, or

b) for the performance of his military duties, shall be punished by imprisonment for one year to five years.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three years to ten years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1

a) and thereby causes serious bodily harm,

b) with a weapon,

c) with at least two persons, or

d) as a member of the armed forces deployed outside the territory of the Slovak Republic.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for ten years to twenty years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1

a) and thereby causes death, or

b) during a crisis situation.

## § 400

Violation of rights and protected interests of soldiers

(1) Whoever coerces another soldier to perform unauthorized personal services or restricts him in his rights, or arbitrarily hinders the performance of his service, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to one year.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 against a subordinate or a person of lower rank.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 or 2

a) by violence, or threat of violence, or threat of other serious harm,

b) with at least two persons or

c) as a member of the armed forces deployed outside the territory of the Slovak Republic.

(4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two years to ten years if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 or 2

a) and thereby causes serious bodily harm or death,

b) by a more serious manner of conduct, or

c) during a crisis situation.

Druhý diel

Criminal Offenses Against the Duty to Perform Military Service

Evading service action or performance of military service

## § 401

(1) Whoever, with the intent to avoid performing an official duty, injures themselves, feigns illness, falsifies a document, abuses an addictive substance or uses another ruse, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to one year.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years if they commit the act specified in paragraph 1 as a member of armed forces deployed outside the territory of the Slovak Republic.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for five to fifteen years if they commit the act specified in paragraph 1 during a crisis situation.

## § 402

(1) Whoever, with the intent to avoid performing military service or fulfilling military duties, injures themselves, feigns illness, falsifies a document, abuses an addictive substance or uses another ruse, or whoever refuses to perform military service or fulfill military duties, shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to five years.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two to eight years if they commit the act specified in paragraph 1 as a member of armed forces deployed outside the territory of the Slovak Republic.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for five to fifteen years if they commit the act specified in paragraph 1 during a crisis situation.

## § 403

Whoever, during service time, by consuming alcohol or other addictive substances, causes a condition that renders them wholly or partially incapable of fulfilling official duties, although they have already been sanctioned at least twice for a similar act in the preceding twelve months, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to one year.

## § 404

Whoever, during a crisis situation, renders themselves incapable of performing service, even through negligence, by consuming alcohol or another addictive substance, shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years.

Desertion

## § 405

(1) Whoever absents themselves without authorization with the intent to avoid military service for a long term or whoever absents themselves without authorization for longer than 30 days, shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to five years.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two to eight years if they commit the act specified in paragraph 1

a) with a weapon, or

b) as a member of armed forces deployed outside the territory of the Slovak Republic.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for five to fifteen years if they commit the act specified in paragraph 1 during a crisis situation.

## § 406

(1) Whoever absents themselves without authorization for longer than 48 hours or absents themselves repeatedly, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to one year.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years if they commit the act specified in paragraph 1 as a member of armed forces deployed outside the territory of the Slovak Republic.

(3) Whoever absents themselves without authorization for longer than 6 days shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to three years.

(4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two to ten years if they absent themselves without authorization for longer than 24 hours during a crisis situation.

(5) Whoever absents themselves without authorization to the enemy shall be punished by imprisonment for five to twelve years.

Part Three

Criminal Offences Against Duties of Guard and Supervisory Service

## § 407

Violation of Duties of Guard Service

(1) Whoever, in guard service or other similar service, violates, even through negligence, the regulations or rules of such service, or special orders issued pursuant thereto, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years if they commit the act specified in paragraph 1

a) during the performance of guard service or other similar service which has particularly important state or military significance,

b) by particularly gross violation of their duty,

c) during a crisis situation, or

d) as a member of armed forces deployed outside the territory of the Slovak Republic.

(3) The same punishment as in paragraph 2 shall apply to whoever causes, by the act specified in paragraph 1, a harmful consequence, the prevention of which the performance of guard service or other similar service was directed toward.

(4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for ten to fifteen years if they intentionally commit the act specified in paragraph 1 during the performance of guard service or other similar service which has particularly important state or military significance during a crisis situation and caused a harmful consequence, the prevention of which the performance of guard service or other similar service was directed toward.

## § 408

Violation of Duties of Supervisory Service

(1) Whoever, in supervisory service or other similar service, in a serious manner, even through negligence, violates the regulations or rules of such service, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to one year.

(2) The same punishment as in paragraph 1 shall apply to whoever, in supervisory service or other similar service, by insufficient fulfillment of their duties, enables the demanding of personal services, restriction of rights or arbitrary hindering of service performance by one soldier against another within the scope of such service.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years if they cause, by the act specified in paragraph 1, a particularly serious consequence which they were obliged to prevent, or commit the act specified in paragraph 1 as a member of armed forces deployed outside the territory of the Slovak Republic.

(4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for one to six years if they commit the act specified in paragraph 1 or 2 during a crisis situation.

## § 409

Violation of Duties in Defence of Airspace

(1) Whoever violates, even through negligence, the regulations or rules of service at radiotechnical stations, in standby units or other facilities designated for ensuring the security of airspace, shall be punished by imprisonment for one to five years.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for three to ten years if they commit the act specified in paragraph 1 and thereby cause a particularly serious consequence.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for eight to fifteen years if they commit the act specified in paragraph 1 during a crisis situation.

Part Four

Criminal Offences Endangering Combat Readiness

## § 410

Endangering the Morale of a Unit

(1) Whoever incites soldiers against military service or against a superior, or whoever otherwise systematically undermines discipline, shall be punished by imprisonment for six months to six years.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for five to fifteen years if they commit the act specified in paragraph 1 during a crisis situation.

## § 411

Violation of Official Duties

(1) Whoever, to the detriment of a component of the armed forces or armed corps, by non-fulfillment of imposed official duties or by their violation, even through negligence, substantially reduces the usability of armament, equipment, other items or financial resources, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to one year.

(2) Whoever, without authorization, uses a tangible asset of small value specified in paragraph 1 or financial resources of small value for a purpose for which it is not intended, or gives consent to such use, or whoever abuses or enables the abuse of subordinates for non-official tasks, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years.

(3) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for two to eight years if they

a) procure for themselves or another a substantial benefit by the act specified in paragraph 2, or

b) cause, by intentionally committed act specified in paragraph 1 or 2, a reduction in combat readiness, substantial damage or another particularly serious consequence.

(4) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for five to fifteen years if they intentionally commit the act specified in paragraph 1 or 2 during a crisis situation.

## § 412

Cowardice Before the Enemy

Whoever, during a combat situation, surrenders into captivity out of cowardice or resignation, shall be punished by imprisonment for eight to fifteen years.

## § 413

Failure to Complete a Combat Task

(1) Whoever absents themselves without permission during a combat situation shall be punished by imprisonment for three to twelve years.

(2) Whoever, during a combat situation, evades the duty to complete a combat task or refuses to use a weapon, shall be punished by imprisonment for ten to fifteen years.

## § 414

Abandonment of Combat Assets

(1) Whoever, during a combat situation, discards, abandons or renders unusable a weapon or other war materiel, shall be punished by imprisonment for two to eight years.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for five to fifteen years if they commit the act specified in paragraph 1 and thereby cause a particularly serious consequence.

## § 415

Surrender of Combat Equipment to the Enemy

A commander who, without it being caused by a combat situation, surrenders to the enemy, even through negligence, military forces or leaves to them fortifications, combat equipment or other combat equipment, shall be punished, if he did not act with the intention to support the enemy, by imprisonment for three to twelve years.

TWELFTH CHAPTER

CRIMINAL OFFENCES AGAINST PEACE, AGAINST HUMANITY, CRIMINAL OFFENCES OF TERRORISM, EXTREMISM AND WAR CRIMES

Part One

Criminal offences against peace and humanity, criminal offences of terrorism and extremism

## § 417

Endangering Peace

(1) Whoever, with the intention to disturb peace, in any way incites to war, propagates war or otherwise supports war propaganda, shall be punished by imprisonment for one to ten years.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for ten to twenty-five years or by life imprisonment if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1

a) in connection with a foreign power or foreign agent,

b) as a member of a dangerous grouping, or

c) during a crisis situation.

## § 418

Genocide

(1) Whoever, with the intention to wholly or partially destroy any nation or any national, ethnic, racial or religious group

a) causes serious bodily harm or death to a member of such group,

b) carries out measures aimed at preventing births within such group,

c) forcibly transfers children from one such group to another, or

d) subjects members of such group to living conditions intended to bring about its complete or partial physical destruction, shall be punished by imprisonment for fifteen to twenty years.

(2) The offender shall be punished by imprisonment for twenty to twenty-five years or by life imprisonment if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1 during war or armed conflict.

(3) The offender shall be punished by life imprisonment if by the act specified in paragraph 1 he causes the death of several persons.

## § 419

Terrorist Attack

(1) Whoever, with the intention to damage the constitutional order or defence capacity of the state, to disrupt or destroy the basic political, economic or social structure of the state or an international organization, to seriously intimidate the population or to compel the government of a state or other public authority or international organization to do, omit or tolerate something

a) threatens to commit or commits an attack endangering life, health or personal liberty of a person,

b) destroys, disables or damages a public facility, transport system, telecommunication system, information system, including serious interference with the functioning of an information system or interruption of the functioning of an information system, fixed platform on the continental shelf, energy facility, water supply facility, medical facility or other important facility, public space or property, or threatens with such act,

c) disrupts, disables or interrupts the supply of water, electricity or other basic natural resource with the aim of exposing people to danger of death or serious bodily harm or foreign property to danger of large-scale damage, or threatens with such act,

d) seizes an aircraft, ship, other means of passenger transport or freight transport or fixed platform on the continental shelf, or exercises control over such means of transport or fixed platform, or destroys or seriously damages a navigation device or interferes with its operation, or communicates false information, thereby endangering the life or health of people, the safety of such means of transport, or exposing foreign property to danger of large-scale damage, or threatens with such act,

e) demands, manufactures, obtains, stores, owns, possesses, imports, exports, transports, has transported, supplies or otherwise uses explosives, nuclear material, radioactive substance, chemical substance, biological agent or toxin, firearm, nuclear weapon, radiological weapon, biological weapon, chemical weapon or other weapon, combat equipment or material of similar nature, or conducts research and development of nuclear weapons, biological weapons, chemical weapons or other weapons or combat equipment or explosives, or device for production, processing, storage or use of nuclear materials, radioactive substances, chemical substances or biological agents and toxins, or threatens with such act, or

f) exposes people to danger of death or serious bodily harm, or foreign property to danger of large-scale damage by causing fire or flood or harmful effects of explosives, gas, electricity or other similarly dangerous substances or forces or commits other similarly dangerous act, or increases such danger or makes its prevention or mitigation more difficult, or threatens with such act, shall be punished by imprisonment for twenty to twenty-five years or by life imprisonment.

(2) The offender shall be punished by life imprisonment if he commits the act specified in paragraph 1

a) and thereby causes serious bodily harm to several persons or death of several persons,

b) on a protected person,

c) against armed forces or against armed corps,

d) as a member of a dangerous grouping, or

e) during a crisis situation.

## § 419a

Participation in Combat Activities of an Organized Armed Group on the Territory of Another State

(1) Whoever during war on the territory of another state actively participates in combat activities of an organized armed group shall be punished by imprisonment for two to eight years.

(2) The same as in paragraph 1 shall be punished whoever

a) publicly incites to commission of the criminal offence specified in paragraph 1,

b) requests another to commit or participate in commission of the act specified in paragraph 1,

c) provides or receives knowledge of methods or techniques for production or use of explosives, firearms or other weapons, harmful substances or other dangerous substances or other special methods or techniques designed for conducting combat for the purpose of committing the act specified in paragraph 1, or

d) provides financial or other means, services, cooperation or creates other conditions for the purpose of committing the criminal offence specified in paragraph 1.

## § 419b

Certain Forms of Participation in Terrorism

(1) Whoever publicly incites to commission of any of the criminal offences of terrorism or publicly approves any of the criminal offences of terrorism shall be punished by imprisonment for three to ten years.

(2) Whoever

a) obtains knowledge of methods or techniques or acquires skills for production, storage, transport or use of explosives, firearms, nuclear weapons, biological weapons, chemical weapons, nuclear materials, radioactive substances, chemical substances, biological agents and toxins or other similarly harmful substances, or dangerous substances for the purpose of committing any of the criminal offences of terrorism,

b) provides knowledge of methods or techniques for production, storage, transport or use of explosives, firearms, nuclear weapons, biological weapons, chemical weapons, nuclear materials, radioactive substances, chemical substances, biological agents and toxins or other similarly harmful substances, or dangerous substances for the purpose of committing any of the criminal offences of terrorism, or

c) requests another to commit or participate in commission of any of the criminal offences of terrorism, shall be punished by imprisonment for seven to fifteen years.

## § 419c

Financing of terrorism

(1) Whoever, by themselves or through another, collects or provides directly or indirectly things, financial means or other means for a perpetrator of terrorism, for a terrorist group, its member, or for the commission of any of the criminal offenses of terrorism, or collects things, financial means or other means with the intention that they can be used in this way, or with the knowledge that they may be used for such purpose, shall be punished by imprisonment for five to fifteen years.

(2) In the same way as in paragraph 1, whoever by themselves or through another provides things, financial means or other means to a close person of someone who participates or participated in the preparation, attempt or commission of any of the criminal offenses of terrorism, for their participation or having participated in such conduct, shall be punished.

(3) By imprisonment for ten to twenty years the perpetrator shall be punished if they commit the act specified in paragraph 1

a) on a larger scale or

b) as a member of a dangerous grouping.

## § 419d

Travel for the purpose of terrorism

(1) Whoever travels from the Slovak Republic or through the territory of the Slovak Republic to another state for the purpose of committing any of the criminal offenses of terrorism, except the criminal offense of terror under § 313 and 314 and the criminal offense of terrorist attack under § 419, shall be punished by imprisonment for five to twelve years.
